# List 30

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>idle</b></td><td>/ˈaɪdl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闲置的，未使用的；懒散的</td><td>idle machines<br>闲置的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Half the factory's machines stood idle for most of last year.  这家工厂一半的机器去年大部分时间都闲置着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dead</td><td>/ˈded/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>死的；毫无生气的</td><td>a dead town at night<br>夜里死寂的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The resort is completely dead in the winter months.  这个度假地在冬季几个月里毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dormant</td><td>/ˈdɔːrmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静止的，不活跃的</td><td>a dormant volcano<br>休眠火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus can stay dormant in the body for years.  这种病毒可在体内潜伏多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fallow</td><td>/ˈfæloʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>休耕的</td><td>leave the field fallow<br>让田地休耕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer left the tired field fallow for a whole season.  农民让这块贫瘠的田地整季休耕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 free</td><td>/ˈfriː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，解放；自由的</td><td>free the hostages<br>释放人质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Negotiators finally persuaded the gunmen to free the hostages.  谈判者最终说服枪手释放了人质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inert</td><td>/ɪˈnɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（人）懒惰缺乏活力的；（物品）惰性的</td><td>an inert gas<br>惰性气体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Helium is an inert gas, so it rarely reacts with other elements.  氦是一种惰性气体，因此很少与其他元素发生反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inoperative</td><td>/ɪnˈɑːpərətɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不起作用的，失效的</td><td>an inoperative rule<br>已失效的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old safety clause in the contract is now inoperative.  合同里那条旧的安全条款现已失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 latent</td><td>/ˈleɪtnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>潜在的，不活跃的</td><td>latent talent<br>潜在的才能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good teacher can discover latent talent in the quietest student.  好老师能在最安静的学生身上发现潜在的才能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unused</td><td>/ʌnˈjuːzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未使用的，闲置的</td><td>an unused back room<br>闲置的后屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two unused machines sat rusting behind the workshop.  两台闲置的机器在车间后面锈着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vacant</td><td>/ˈveɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茫然的，无表情的；空的</td><td>a vacant expression<br>茫然的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered with a vacant stare that unsettled everyone present.  他以茫然的目光作答，让在场的人都不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indolent</td><td>/ˈɪndələnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>an indolent young man<br>懒散的年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too indolent to look for work, he simply lay on the couch all summer.  他懒得去找工作，整个夏天就躺在沙发上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lazy</td><td>/ˈleɪzi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>too lazy to cook<br>懒得做饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too lazy to walk the dog on cold mornings.  寒冷的早晨他懒得去遛狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shiftless</td><td>/ˈʃɪftləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>胸无大志的，懒惰的</td><td>a shiftless drifter<br>游手好闲的流浪者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His father dismissed him as a shiftless young man without ambition.  他父亲斥他为没有志向、游手好闲的年轻人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slothful</td><td>/ˈsloʊθfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的，迟缓的</td><td>slothful habits<br>懒散的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of easy living had made the once-diligent clerk slothful.  多年安逸生活使这位曾经勤勉的职员变得懒散。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dally</td><td>/ˈdæli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度时光，磨蹭</td><td>dally over lunch<br>吃午饭磨蹭半天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't dally on the way home; dinner is almost ready.  回家路上别磨蹭，晚饭快好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dillydally</del></td><td>/ˈdɪlidæli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>磨蹭，浪费时间</td><td>dillydally on the way<br>在路上磨蹭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stop dillydallying and finish your homework before dinner is served.  别磨蹭了，晚饭前把作业写完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drone</td><td>/droʊn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>低沉单调地说；嗡嗡地叫</td><td>drone on about details<br>唠唠叨叨讲细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker droned on for another twenty minutes.  演讲者又单调地讲了二十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laze</td><td>/leɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>懒散度日，闲躺</td><td>laze in the sun<br>在阳光下懒躺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lazed by the pool all Sunday afternoon.  他们整个周日下午都在池边懒躺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loaf</td><td>/ˈloʊf/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>游荡，闲混</td><td>loaf around all day<br>整天闲混</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boys loafed by the river instead of working.  这些男孩没干活，在河边闲混。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loll</td><td>/lɑːl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>懒躺，闲混度日</td><td>loll on the sofa<br>懒躺在沙发上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children lolled on the sofa instead of finishing their homework.  孩子们懒躺在沙发上，而不是把作业做完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lounge</td><td>/ˈlaʊndʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>懒散地待着，闲荡</td><td>lounge by the pool<br>在泳池边闲躺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They lounged in the shade all afternoon without doing anything.  他们整个下午在阴凉处闲躺，什么也没做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 active</td><td>/ˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>活跃的，活动的</td><td>an active volcano<br>活火山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stays active by swimming every morning.  她每天早上游泳以保持活跃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alive</td><td>/əˈlaɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活着的，存在的</td><td>keep the tradition alive<br>让传统延续下去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The language is still alive in a few remote mountain villages.  这门语言在少数偏远山村中仍然活着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 employed</td><td>/emˈplɔɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>在职的；被使用的</td><td>gainfully employed workers<br>有薪就业的工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nearly every graduate was employed within six months.  几乎每位毕业生半年内都找到了工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 functioning</td><td>/ˈfʌŋkʃənɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>运转中的，正常运作的</td><td>a fully functioning system<br>完全正常运转的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only one functioning elevator served the entire building.  整栋楼只有一台电梯还在运转。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 operative</td><td>/ˈɑːpərətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有效的，起作用的</td><td>the operative rule here<br>此处有效的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The older statute is no longer operative in this state.  那部旧法规在本州已不再有效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 running</td><td>/ˈrʌnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>连续的，不断的</td><td>a running commentary<br>连续的解说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept a running argument going for the whole journey.  他们一路上争论不休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 working</td><td>/ˈwɜːrkɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>工作中的，运转的</td><td>a working printer<br>能用的打印机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We finally found a working phone in the lobby.  我们终于在大厅找到一部能用的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diligent</td><td>/ˈdɪlɪdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，辛勤的</td><td>a diligent student<br>勤奋的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A diligent editor checked every footnote before the manuscript went to the printer.  一位勤勉的编辑在书稿付印前核对了每一条脚注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 industrious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌstriəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>勤勉的，刻苦的</td><td>an industrious worker<br>勤劳的工人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most industrious student in the class reviewed her notes every single night.  班里最勤奋的学生每晚都复习笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>illbred</b></del></td><td>/ˌɪlˈbred/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有教养的，没有礼貌的</td><td>an illbred remark<br>没礼貌的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Interrupting an elder in that manner struck everyone as illbred.  那样打断长辈说话让所有人觉得没有教养。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discourteous</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>a discourteous reply<br>无礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was discourteous to leave without thanking the host.  不向主人道谢就离开是无礼的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disrespectful</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈspektfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不尊重的，无礼的</td><td>disrespectful behavior<br>不尊重人的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Interrupting your teacher like that is disrespectful.  那样打断老师说话是不尊重人的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impertinent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrtnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不相关的；无礼的，鲁莽的</td><td>an impertinent remark<br>无礼的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer objected that the question was impertinent to the charges.  律师反对说，这个问题与指控无关。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impolite</td><td>/ˌɪmpəˈlaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>an impolite remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is impolite to interrupt someone in the middle of a sentence.  在别人话没说完时插嘴很不礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inconsiderate</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不体谅他人的</td><td>inconsiderate neighbors<br>不体谅人的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Playing loud music at midnight is simply inconsiderate.  半夜大声放音乐实在是不体谅别人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rude</td><td>/ˈruːd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗鲁的，无礼的</td><td>a rude remark<br>无礼的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was rude of him to leave without thanking the host.  他不谢主人就离开，很没礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncivil</td><td>/ʌnˈsɪvəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不礼貌的，粗鲁的</td><td>an uncivil remark<br>粗鲁的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clerk's uncivil answer made the customer complain.  职员粗鲁的回答让客户投诉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ungracious</td><td>/ʌnˈɡreɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，不客气的</td><td>an ungracious refusal<br>不客气的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed ungracious to refuse such a generous offer.  拒绝如此大方的提议似乎显得无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unmannerly</del></td><td>/ʌnˈmænərli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，粗鲁的</td><td>unmannerly behavior at table<br>餐桌上的无礼举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unmannerly interruptions annoyed everyone at the meeting.  他无礼的插话让会上所有人不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 civil</td><td>/ˈsɪvəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的；民事的</td><td>a civil reply<br>有礼的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to stay civil even when the discussion gets heated.  即使讨论变得激烈也要努力保持礼貌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 considerate</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体谅的，考虑周到的</td><td>considerate of others<br>体谅他人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was considerate of her to call before visiting.  她来访前先打电话，很体贴周到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 courteous</td><td>/ˈkɜːrtiəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>礼貌的，谦恭有礼的</td><td>a courteous reply<br>礼貌的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even when customers shouted, the young clerk remained calm and courteous.  即使顾客大声嚷嚷，那位年轻店员仍然平静而有礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gracious</td><td>/ˈɡreɪʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，亲切的</td><td>a gracious host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a gracious host who made every guest feel welcome.  她是位亲切的主人，让每位客人都感到受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mannerly</td><td>/ˈmænərli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有礼貌的，举止得体的</td><td>a mannerly child<br>有礼貌的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mannerly boy thanked everyone before he left the table.  这个有礼貌的男孩离席前向每个人道谢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 polite</td><td>/pəˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有礼貌的，客气的</td><td>make polite conversation<br>客套地寒暄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is polite to greet your neighbors in the morning.  早上向邻居问好是有礼貌的做法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 urbane</td><td>/ərˈbeɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>彬彬有礼的，文雅的</td><td>an urbane host<br>文雅的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our urbane host guided the conversation with easy wit and grace.  我们文雅的主人以轻松的机智和风度引导着谈话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 well-bred</td><td>/wɛl ˈbrɛd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，有礼貌的</td><td>a well-bred young woman<br>一位有教养的年轻女子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The well-bred guest thanked the host before leaving.  这位有教养的客人离开前向主人道了谢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>illustrious</b></td><td>/ɪˈlʌstriəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，杰出的</td><td>an illustrious career<br>辉煌杰出的生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She retired after an illustrious career in medical research.  在医学研究领域度过杰出的生涯后，她退休了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguished</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卓越的，杰出的；高贵的</td><td>a distinguished scholar<br>杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize went to a distinguished historian of medieval law.  奖项授予一位杰出的中世纪法律史学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luminous</td><td>/ˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，明亮的</td><td>a luminous dial<br>夜光表盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luminous dial of his watch glowed faintly in the dark.  他手表的夜光表盘在黑暗中微微发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noble</td><td>/ˈnoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>高贵的，贵族的</td><td>a noble bearing<br>高贵的气派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 notable</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显著的；值得注意的</td><td>a notable achievement<br>显著的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has been a notable drop in traffic accidents this year.  今年交通事故有显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noteworthy</td><td>/ˈnoʊˌtwɜːrði/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>值得注意的，显著的</td><td>a noteworthy achievement<br>一项值得注意的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most noteworthy feature of the report is its honesty.  这份报告最值得注意的地方是它的诚实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outstanding</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈstændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的，突出的</td><td>an outstanding performance<br>出色的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an outstanding performance in her very first concert.  她在首场音乐会上表现出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preeminent</td><td>/priˈemɪnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>优秀的，重要的</td><td>the preeminent scholar<br>最杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is the preeminent authority on medieval trade routes in Europe.  他是研究欧洲中世纪贸易路线最杰出的权威。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prestigious</td><td>/preˈstɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有声望的，受尊敬的</td><td>a prestigious award<br>有声望的奖项</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He turned down a prestigious post to stay near his family.  他为了留在家人身边拒绝了一个有声望的职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redoubtable</td><td>/rɪˈdaʊtəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的，值得尊敬的；可怕的</td><td>a redoubtable rival<br>令人敬畏的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced a redoubtable rival in the final round of the tournament.  她在锦标赛决赛轮中遇上一位令人敬畏的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 signal</td><td>/ˈsɪɡnəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>（在重要性、成就方面）非同寻常的；给出信号</td><td>a signal achievement<br>非同寻常的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Landing the probe safely was a signal achievement for the young team.  探测器安全着陆是这支年轻团队非同寻常的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 average</td><td>/ˈævərɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平庸的，一般的；平均数</td><td>an average student<br>成绩平平的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His work is average but it always arrives on time.  他的活儿一般，但总能按时交。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inferior</td><td>/ɪnˈfɪriər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>低等的，次等的</td><td>inferior in quality<br>质量较差</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Customers complained that the new fabric was clearly inferior.  顾客抱怨新面料明显质量较差。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mediocre</td><td>/ˌmiːdiˈoʊkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸的；质量中等偏下的</td><td>a mediocre performance<br>平庸的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra's mediocre performance disappointed listeners who had waited months for the concert.  乐团平庸的表演让等了几个月的听众很失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>imperishable</b></td><td>/ɪmˈperɪʃəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不朽的，永恒的</td><td>imperishable glory<br>不朽的荣耀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldiers won imperishable glory defending the bridge for three days.  士兵们守桥三天赢得了不朽的荣耀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ageless</td><td>/ˈeɪdʒləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永不显老的，永恒的</td><td>ageless beauty<br>不老的美貌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ageless beauty surprised everyone at the reunion.  她不显岁月的美貌让聚会上的人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continuing</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>持续的，延续的</td><td>a continuing debate<br>持续不断的争论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute remains a continuing source of tension.  这场纠纷仍是持续的紧张来源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immortal</td><td>/ɪˈmɔːrtəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不朽的，永生的</td><td>immortal fame<br>不朽的声名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's immortal lines are still quoted two thousand years later.  这位诗人不朽的诗句两千年后仍被引用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lasting</td><td>/ˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，永久的</td><td>a lasting impression<br>持久的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her kindness left a lasting impression on everyone in the ward.  她的善意给病房里每个人留下持久印象。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perennial</td><td>/pəˈreniəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>长期的，反复出现的</td><td>a perennial problem<br>长期存在的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic is a perennial problem in this old city.  交通是这座老城长期存在的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 timeless</td><td>/ˈtaɪmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不受时间影响的</td><td>timeless design<br>经典不过时的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dress has a timeless elegance that never dates.  这条裙子有一种永不过时的优雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undying</td><td>/ʌnˈdaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不灭的</td><td>undying love<br>永恒的爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pledged her undying loyalty to the cause she had joined.  她宣誓对所投身的事业永葆忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fleeting</td><td>/ˈfliːtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；转瞬即逝的</td><td>a fleeting moment<br>短暂的瞬间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For one fleeting moment the clouds parted above the valley.  有一个短暂的瞬间，云在山谷上方散开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transient</td><td>/ˈtrænʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的；暂时的；过往的</td><td>transient population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame in the internet age is often transient and quickly forgotten.  网络时代的名声往往短暂，很快被人遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 transitory</td><td>/ˈtrænsətɔːri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的</td><td>transitory pleasure<br>短暂的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His anger was transitory and he apologized within the hour.  他的愤怒很短暂，一小时内就道了歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>implicit</b></td><td>/ɪmˈplɪsɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不言而喻的，心照不宣的</td><td>an implicit agreement<br>心照不宣的协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Club members share an implicit agreement to split all travel costs.  俱乐部成员之间有一个心照不宣的约定：平摊所有旅行开销。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implied</td><td>/ɪmˈplaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暗示的，含蓄的</td><td>an implied threat<br>暗含的威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His polite note carried an implied threat that everyone recognized.  他礼貌的字条里含着人人都听得出的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unexpressed</td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈsprɛst/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未说出的，未表露的</td><td>unexpressed doubts<br>未说出的疑虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unexpressed doubts surfaced only after the contract was signed.  她未说出的疑虑直到合同签署后才浮现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unspoken</td><td>/ʌnˈspoʊkən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未说出口的，心照不宣的</td><td>an unspoken agreement<br>不言而喻的约定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was an unspoken rule that nobody mentioned his first marriage.  有条不言而喻的规矩：没人提他的第一次婚姻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unvoiced</td><td>/ʌnˈvɔɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未表达出来的</td><td>unvoiced objections<br>未说出的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several unvoiced objections finally appeared in the written survey.  几项未说出口的反对最终出现在书面问卷中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wordless</td><td>/ˈwɜːrdləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无言的，不用言语的</td><td>a wordless glance<br>无言的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They exchanged a wordless glance and left the room.  他们交换了无言的一瞥便离开房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explicit</td><td>/ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，明确的</td><td>explicit instructions<br>明确的指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave explicit instructions about how to store the samples.  她就样品的保存方式给出了明确指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expressed</td><td>/ɪkˈsprest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确表达的</td><td>the expressed wishes<br>明确表达的意愿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They acted against the clearly expressed wishes of the committee.  他们的行动违背了委员会明确表达的意愿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spoken</td><td>/ˈspoʊkən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>口头说出的，口语的</td><td>a spoken agreement<br>口头协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A spoken promise carried little weight without anything in writing.  没有书面记录，口头承诺没什么分量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stated</td><td>/ˈsteɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确陈述的，规定的</td><td>the stated purpose<br>明确宣称的目的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stated purpose of the trip was research, not tourism.  这次出行明确宣称的目的是研究，而非旅游。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 voiced</td><td>/ˈvɔɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>说出的，表达出来的</td><td>openly voiced concerns<br>公开表达的担忧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Openly voiced concerns forced the school to change its plan.  公开表达的担忧迫使学校改变了计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>impunity</b></td><td>/ɪmˈpjuːnəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>免责，免受处罚</td><td>act with total impunity<br>肆意妄为而不受罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For years the smugglers operated with almost complete impunity.  多年来，走私者几乎完全不受处罚地活动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exemption</td><td>/ɪɡˈzempʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>豁免，免除</td><td>claim a tax exemption<br>申请税收减免</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students may apply for an exemption from the fee.  学生可以申请免缴这笔费用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immunity</td><td>/ɪˈmjuːnəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>豁免；免疫力</td><td>diplomatic immunity<br>外交豁免权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness testified after being granted immunity.  证人在获得豁免后作证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liability</td><td>/ˌlaɪəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>责任</td><td>limited liability<br>有限责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract clearly limits the company's liability for accidental damage.  合同明确限制了公司对意外损害承担的责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incentive</b></td><td>/ɪnˈsentɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激，诱因</td><td>a tax incentive<br>税收上的激励</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tax breaks gave farmers a strong incentive to plant native trees.  税收减免给了农民种植本土树木的强烈诱因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goad</td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impetus</td><td>/ˈɪmpətəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>推动力，促进因素</td><td>give fresh impetus to<br>为……提供新动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The grant gave fresh impetus to their stalled research.  这笔资助为他们停滞的研究提供了新动力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incitement</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪtmənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动，刺激因素</td><td>incitement to violence<br>对暴力的煽动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with incitement to riot after the speech.  演讲之后他被控煽动骚乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigation</td><td>/ˌɪnstɪˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动，教唆</td><td>at his instigation<br>在他的教唆下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike began at the instigation of two union leaders.  罢工是在两位工会领袖的煽动下开始的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 momentum</td><td>/moʊˈmentəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>动力；势头</td><td>gather momentum<br>积聚势头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign gathered momentum after the debate and won three states easily.  这场竞选在辩论后积聚了势头，轻松赢下三个州。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motivation</td><td>/ˌmoʊtəˈveɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>动机，动力</td><td>lack of motivation<br>缺乏动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor pay explains the team's total lack of motivation.  报酬低解释了团队完全缺乏动力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motive</td><td>/ˈmoʊtɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>动机，原因</td><td>a clear financial motive<br>明确的金钱动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police could find no obvious motive for the theft.  警方找不到这起盗窃的明显动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provocation</td><td>/ˌprɑːvəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激，挑动；激怒</td><td>without any provocation<br>毫无缘由地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog bit him without the slightest provocation.  那狗毫无缘由就咬了他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spur</td><td>/spɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物</td><td>a spur to growth<br>增长的刺激因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising exports acted as a powerful spur to industrial growth.  出口增长成为工业发展的强大刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulant</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>兴奋剂，刺激物</td><td>a mild stimulant<br>温和的兴奋剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coffee acts as a stimulant for most healthy adults.  对大多数健康成年人来说咖啡起兴奋剂作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulus</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物，促进因素</td><td>an economic stimulus<br>经济刺激措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap loans acted as a stimulus to small businesses.  低息贷款成为小企业的刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 yeast</td><td>/ˈjiːst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>酵母；引发变化之物</td><td>the yeast of new ideas<br>催生变化的新思想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap printing became the yeast of political change.  廉价印刷成了政治变革的催化剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deterrent</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>遏制因素，威慑物</td><td>a strong deterrent<br>强有力的威慑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fines act as a deterrent to careless drivers.  重罚对粗心司机起到威慑作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disincentive</td><td>/ˌdɪsɪnˈsentɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>抑制因素，负激励</td><td>a disincentive to invest<br>对投资的抑制因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High taxes act as a disincentive to small businesses.  高税收对小企业起到抑制作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>incumbent</b></td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>义不容辞的，必须的</td><td>incumbent on the manager<br>经理义不容辞的事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is incumbent on every driver to check the brakes regularly.  定期检查刹车是每位司机义不容辞的责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compulsory</td><td>/kəmˈpʌlsəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制的，必修的</td><td>compulsory education<br>义务教育</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing a helmet is compulsory on every construction site.  在每个建筑工地戴安全帽都是强制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forced</td><td>/ˈfɔːrst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>被迫的，强迫的</td><td>a forced confession<br>被迫的供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court threw out the forced confession as unreliable.  法庭以不可靠为由驳回了这份被迫的供词。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imperative</td><td>/ɪmˈperətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>命令的，强制性的；迫切的</td><td>an imperative need<br>迫切的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is imperative that we repair the dam before the rains arrive.  在雨季来临前修好大坝是迫切必需的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 involuntary</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非自愿的；无意识的</td><td>an involuntary reaction<br>不自觉的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Blinking is an involuntary response to sudden light.  眨眼是对突然强光的不自觉反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 necessary</td><td>/ˈnesəˌseri/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>必需的，必要的；必需品</td><td>a necessary expense<br>一项必要的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clean water is necessary for even the simplest household.  即便最简朴的家庭也需要干净的水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obligatory</td><td>/əˈblɪɡətɔːri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强制性的</td><td>obligatory military service<br>强制的兵役</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In that country obligatory military service lasts for eighteen months.  在那个国家，强制的兵役要服十八个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peremptory</td><td>/pəˈremptəri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的；专横傲慢的</td><td>a peremptory order<br>不容置疑的命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His peremptory tone left no room for questions or discussion.  他傲慢的语气不给任何提问或讨论的余地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 required</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>必需的，规定的</td><td>required reading<br>必读材料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A signed form is required before the trip can begin.  出行前必须提交一份签好的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elective</td><td>/ɪˈlektɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可自由选择的；选举的</td><td>an elective course<br>选修课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of the surgery performed here is elective, not urgent.  这里做的多数手术是可选择的，而非紧急的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 optional</td><td>/ˈɑːpʃənəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可选择的，非强制的</td><td>an optional extra course<br>一门选修的额外课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely optional.  参加晚间研讨会完全是自愿的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 voluntary</td><td>/ˈvɑːlənteri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自愿的，志愿的</td><td>voluntary participation<br>自愿参与</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Attendance at the evening seminar is entirely voluntary.  晚间研讨会完全自愿参加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>indecorous</b></td><td>/ɪnˈdekərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合乎礼节的，不得体的</td><td>indecorous remarks at the ceremony<br>典礼上不得体的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His indecorous jokes at the memorial service embarrassed nearly everyone in the room.  他在追悼会上不得体的玩笑让在场几乎所有人都很尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amiss</td><td>/əˈmɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>出错的，不对劲的</td><td>something is amiss<br>有些不对劲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sensed at once that something was seriously amiss at home.  她立刻感到家里出了严重问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 graceless</td><td>/ˈɡreɪsləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨拙的，不优雅的</td><td>a graceless heavy stride<br>笨重不雅的步态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His graceless attempt at an apology made things worse.  他笨拙的道歉尝试让事情更糟了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 improper</td><td>/ˌɪmˈprɑːpər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适当的，不合礼节的</td><td>improper use of funds<br>资金使用不当</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was dismissed for improper conduct toward a colleague.  他因对同事行为不当而被解雇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 inapposite</del></td><td>/ɪnˈæpəzɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不恰当的，不相关的</td><td>an inapposite comparison<br>不恰当的比较</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comparison to wartime rationing seems inapposite today.  把它比作战时配给在今天显得不恰当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 infelicitous</del></td><td>/ɪnfɪˈlɪsɪtəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不愉快的，不幸的；不适当的，不合时宜的</td><td>an infelicitous remark<br>不合时宜的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His infelicitous remark about her family ruined the otherwise pleasant dinner party.  他关于她家庭的不合时宜的话毁了本来愉快的晚宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 malapropos</del></td><td>/ˌmæləprəˈpoʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>不合时宜的</td><td>a malapropos joke<br>不合时宜的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His malapropos comment about money embarrassed everyone at dinner.  他关于钱的不合时宜的评论让餐桌上所有人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perverse</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反常固执的，任性的</td><td>take perverse pleasure<br>反常地感到快意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He takes perverse pleasure in arguing against every popular opinion.  他反常地乐于反驳每一个流行观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbecoming</td><td>/ˌʌnbɪˈkʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，不合适的</td><td>conduct unbecoming an officer<br>不合军官身份的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such language is unbecoming in a public hearing.  这种言辞在公开听证会上不得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfit</td><td>/ʌnˈfɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不适合的；不健康的</td><td>unfit for human consumption<br>不适合人类食用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old bridge was declared unfit for heavy traffic.  这座老桥被判定不适合重型车辆通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unseemly</td><td>/ʌnˈsiːmli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不得体的，不适宜的</td><td>unseemly haste<br>失礼的匆忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their unseemly quarrel spoiled the whole wedding reception.  他们不得体的争吵毁了整场婚宴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsuitable</td><td>/ʌnˈsuːtəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合适的，不适宜的</td><td>unsuitable for children<br>不适合儿童</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soil here is completely unsuitable for growing rice.  这里的土壤完全不适合种稻子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wrong</td><td>/ˈrɔːŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不道德的，错误的</td><td>morally wrong<br>道德上错误的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone agreed the decision was simply wrong.  所有人都认为那个决定根本就是错的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 appropriate</td><td>/əˈproʊprieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>私自挪用；适当的</td><td>appropriate public funds<br>挪用公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials were accused of appropriating public money for private use.  官员们被指控将公款挪为私用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 becoming</td><td>/bɪˈkʌmɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的，得体的</td><td>a becoming dress<br>得体的裙子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her modest reply was becoming in such a formal setting.  在如此正式的场合，她谦逊的回答很得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 befitting</td><td>/bɪˈfɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>适当的，相称的</td><td>a welcome befitting a hero<br>配得上英雄的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They gave him a farewell befitting a long and honest career.  他们为他举行了与漫长诚实的职业生涯相称的告别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decorous</td><td>/ˈdekərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体端正的</td><td>decorous behavior at dinner<br>晚餐时得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests kept a decorous silence while the old bishop said grace.  老主教做餐前祷告时，客人们保持着得体的安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 felicitous</td><td>/fɪˈlɪsətəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>恰当的，贴切的</td><td>a felicitous phrase<br>贴切的措辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The title is a felicitous choice for such a book.  这个书名对这样一本书来说是贴切的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fit</td><td>/ˈfɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>相符，适合；合适的</td><td>fit the description<br>符合描述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The suspect they arrested did not fit the witness's description.  他们逮捕的嫌疑人并不符合证人的描述。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genteel</td><td>/dʒenˈtiːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有教养的，不粗俗的</td><td>genteel manners<br>有教养的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 meet</td><td>/miːt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的</td><td>a meet response<br>合适的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A public apology seemed the only meet response to such a serious blunder.  公开道歉似乎是对如此严重失误唯一合适的回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 proper</td><td>/ˈprɑːpər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>适当的，正确的</td><td>the proper procedure<br>正确的程序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please store the samples in the proper containers.  请把样本存放在合适的容器里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 seemly</td><td>/ˈsiːmli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>得体的，合宜的</td><td>seemly conduct in public<br>公共场合得体的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher expects seemly conduct from her students in public places.  老师期望学生在公共场所举止得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 suitable</td><td>/ˈsuːtəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>合适的，适宜的</td><td>suitable for beginners<br>适合初学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This film is not suitable for very young children.  这部影片不适合幼儿观看。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>infraction</b></td><td>/ɪnˈfrækʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违背，违犯</td><td>a minor infraction<br>轻微的违规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee stopped play to penalize a minor infraction near the goal.  裁判中断比赛，对球门附近的轻微违规进行判罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breach</td><td>/briːtʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>违背</td><td>breach the contract<br>违约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company breached the contract by delivering the goods late.  这家公司延迟交货，违反了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contravention</td><td>/ˌkɑːntrəˈvenʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违反，违犯</td><td>in contravention of the rules<br>违反规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dumping was a clear contravention of local law.  这次倾倒明显违反了当地法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infringement</td><td>/ɪnˈfrɪndʒmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵犯，违反</td><td>copyright infringement<br>版权侵犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ruled that the logo was a clear infringement of copyright.  法院裁定该标志明显侵犯了版权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transgression</td><td>/trænzˈɡreʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违法，越界行为</td><td>a minor transgression<br>轻微的违规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He apologized publicly for that youthful transgression.  他为年轻时的那次违规公开道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trespass</td><td>/ˈtrespəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>擅自进入；侵入；侵犯</td><td>trespass on private land<br>非法侵入私人土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hikers who trespass on the ranch will be fined by the county.  擅自进入这座牧场的徒步者会被县里罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 violation</td><td>/vaɪəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违反，侵犯</td><td>a violation of the rules<br>违反规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parking here is a direct violation of the city ordinance.  在此停车直接违反了市政条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 observance</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrvəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>遵守；（仪式的）举行</td><td>strict observance of tradition<br>严格遵守传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village is known for its faithful observance of ancient customs.  这个村庄以忠实遵守古老习俗著称。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>justify</b></td><td>/ˈdʒʌstɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>证明…的合理性，为…辩解</td><td>justify his decision<br>证明他的决定合理</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing in the report can justify his decision to conceal the results.  报告里没有任何内容能证明他隐瞒结果的决定是合理的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excuse</td><td>/ɪkˈskjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>为…辩解，宽恕；借口</td><td>excuse his rudeness<br>为他的粗鲁辩解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing can excuse such careless handling of the evidence.  如此草率地处理证据是无法辩解的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rationalize</td><td>/ˈræʃənəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…找借口，使合理化</td><td>rationalize his own failure<br>为自己的失败找借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rationalized the purchase as an urgent necessity.  她把这次购买说成迫切必需，为自己找借口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lag</b></td><td>/læɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>落后，行动缓慢</td><td>lag behind the group<br>落在队伍后面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The youngest hiker lagged far behind the group on the steep trail.  最年幼的徒步者在陡峭的小道上远远落在队伍后面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crawl</td><td>/ˈkrɔːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>缓慢行进；爬行</td><td>crawl along the highway<br>在公路上缓慢行进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic crawled along the flooded highway for nearly three hours.  车流在被淹的公路上缓慢行进了近三个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 creep</td><td>/kriːp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>缓慢地行进</td><td>creep along slowly<br>缓慢地爬行前进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic crept forward only a few meters at a time all morning.  整个上午车流每次只缓慢前进几米。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dally</td><td>/ˈdæli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度时光，磨蹭</td><td>dally over lunch<br>吃午饭磨蹭半天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't dally on the way home; dinner is almost ready.  回家路上别磨蹭，晚饭快好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dawdle</td><td>/ˈdɔːdl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拖拖拉拉，磨蹭</td><td>dawdle on the way<br>路上拖拖拉拉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dawdled over her homework until it was nearly midnight.  她做作业拖拖拉拉，一直到近午夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 dillydally</del></td><td>/ˈdɪlidæli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>磨蹭，浪费时间</td><td>dillydally on the way<br>在路上磨蹭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Stop dillydallying and finish your homework before dinner is served.  别磨蹭了，晚饭前把作业写完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 linger</td><td>/ˈlɪŋɡər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>磨蹭，闲荡</td><td>linger in the doorway<br>在门口徘徊磨蹭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests lingered in the doorway long after the party had officially ended.  聚会正式结束后很久，客人们还在门口磨蹭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loiter</td><td>/ˈlɔɪtər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闲荡，徘徊</td><td>loiter near the entrance<br>在入口附近闲荡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teenagers loitered outside the shop until the owner finally complained.  少年们在店外闲荡，直到店主终于抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 poke</td><td>/ˈpoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>缓慢行进，闲荡</td><td>poke along the road<br>沿路慢慢晃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old truck poked along the highway at thirty miles an hour.  那辆旧卡车以每小时三十英里在公路上慢慢晃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tarry</td><td>/ˈtæri/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逗留，耽搁</td><td>tarry no longer<br>不再逗留</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not tarry here; the last train leaves in minutes.  别在这儿磨蹭，末班车几分钟后就开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decay</td><td>/dɪˈkeɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>衰败，腐烂</td><td>fall into slow decay<br>逐渐衰败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The abandoned mill has been left to decay for decades.  这座废弃磨坊已任其衰败数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 droop</td><td>/ˈdruːp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低垂，萎靡</td><td>the flowers begin to droop<br>花开始低垂萎蔫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plants drooped badly after three days without water.  三天没浇水，植物都严重萎蔫低垂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 emaciate</del></td><td>/ɪˈmeɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使消瘦，使憔悴</td><td>emaciate the patient's body<br>使病人身体消瘦衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of fever had emaciated the patient's body almost beyond recognition.  数月高烧使病人的身体消瘦衰弱得几乎认不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fade</td><td>/ˈfeɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，消退；褪色</td><td>hopes began to fade<br>希望开始消退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hopes of rescue began to fade after three days.  三天后他们获救的希望开始消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fail</td><td>/ˈfeɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>衰退，衰竭；失败</td><td>his strength failed<br>他体力衰竭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His eyesight began to fail in his early seventies.  他七十出头时视力开始衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languish</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>变得衰弱</td><td>languish in a damp cell<br>在潮湿牢房中日渐衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prisoners languished in damp cells for months without proper food.  囚犯们在潮湿的牢房里日渐衰弱，数月没有适当的食物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sink</td><td>/ˈsɪŋk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>下沉，下降；水槽</td><td>sink below the horizon<br>沉到地平线下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old boat began to sink soon after the storm.  暴风雨过后不久那条旧船开始下沉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waste</td><td>/ˈweɪst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词、动词</td><td>荒芜的；浪费；废物</td><td>waste land<br>荒地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing grows on this waste ground beside the old factory.  旧工厂旁的这片荒地上什么也长不出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wilt</td><td>/wɪlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>精神萎靡，憔悴</td><td>wilt in the heat<br>在酷热中萎靡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The runners began to wilt in the heat of the final mile.  跑到最后一英里的酷热中，选手们开始萎靡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wither</td><td>/ˈwɪðər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>枯萎，凋谢</td><td>wither in the heat<br>在高温中枯萎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomato plants withered after a week without rain or shade.  一周无雨又没有遮荫，番茄苗都枯萎了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mope</td><td>/ˈmoʊp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闷闷不乐，郁闷</td><td>mope around the house<br>在屋里闷闷不乐地转</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She moped around the house for days after the news.  听到消息后她在屋里闷闷不乐地转了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blast</td><td>/blæst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>爆炸；斥责，抨击</td><td>a bomb blast<br>炸弹爆炸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bomb blast shattered every window on the narrow street.  一次炸弹爆炸震碎了这条窄街上的每扇窗户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bolt</td><td>/ˈboʊlt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>突然说出；猛冲</td><td>bolt out an answer<br>脱口说出答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bolted out the secret before anyone could stop him.  他还没等人阻止就把秘密说了出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bustle</td><td>/ˈbʌsl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>忙乱，喧闹；（快速地）行走，奔忙</td><td>the bustle of the street<br>街上的喧闹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love the bustle of the market on Saturday mornings.  我喜欢周六早晨市场上的喧闹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careen</td><td>/kəˈriːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>摇晃疾行，横冲直撞</td><td>careen down the hill<br>摇晃着冲下山坡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty cart careened down the hill and struck a fence.  空车摇晃着冲下山坡，撞上了篱笆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 career</td><td>/kəˈrɪr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>疾驰，飞奔</td><td>career down the hill<br>沿山坡疾驰而下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The empty cart careered down the slope and hit a fence.  空车沿坡疾冲而下撞上了栅栏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dash</td><td>/ˈdæʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>猛冲，疾奔</td><td>dash across the street<br>快速冲过街道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dashed across the street to catch the departing bus.  她冲过街道去赶那辆即将开走的公交车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fly</td><td>/ˈflaɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>飞奔，疾行；飞</td><td>fly down the street<br>沿街飞奔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She flew down the corridor to catch the departing train.  她飞奔穿过走廊去赶将要开走的火车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hustle</td><td>/ˈhʌsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>催促；奋力行进</td><td>hustle through the crowd<br>在人群中挤行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hustled the children into the car before the rain.  她在下雨前把孩子们匆匆赶上车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rush</td><td>/rʌʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙，急速行动；催促</td><td>rush to the airport<br>赶往机场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed to the airport and barely caught the flight.  他们赶往机场，勉强赶上了航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rustle</td><td>/ˈrʌsl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>快速地行动</td><td>rustle up a meal<br>快手弄出一顿饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She rustled up a hot meal for six guests in twenty minutes.  她二十分钟内为六位客人麻利地做出一顿热饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 speed</td><td>/ˈspiːd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>快速行进，疾驰</td><td>speed down the highway<br>在公路上疾驰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambulance sped down the empty highway toward the hospital.  救护车沿着空旷的公路疾驰驶向医院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 whirl</td><td>/ˈwɜːrl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>急转，飞旋；旋转</td><td>whirl around<br>猛地转过身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Snowflakes whirled madly in the beam of the streetlight.  雪花在路灯光柱中疯狂飞旋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 convalesce</td><td>/ˌkɑːnvəˈles/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>渐渐康复，渐愈</td><td>convalesce at home<br>在家中养病康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spent three quiet weeks convalescing at home after the surgery.  手术后她在家静养康复了三周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rally</td><td>/ˈræli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>恢复，重振；集合；集会</td><td>rally after surgery<br>手术后恢复过来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient rallied surprisingly quickly after a week of treatment.  经过一周治疗，病人恢复得出人意料地快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebound</td><td>/riˈbaʊnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>反弹，回升</td><td>rebound from a slump<br>从低迷中回升</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sales rebounded sharply in the final three months.  销售在最后三个月大幅回升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recover</td><td>/rɪˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>康复；恢复，找回</td><td>recover from illness<br>病后康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took him two months to recover from the serious operation.  他花了两个月才从这场大手术中康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recuperate</td><td>/rɪˈkuːpəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>康复，恢复（健康或体力）</td><td>recuperate from an illness<br>病后康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent two weeks at the coast recuperating from surgery.  他在海边待了两周，从手术中康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lance</b></td><td>/læns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺穿，刺破</td><td>lance an infected boil<br>刺破感染的脓肿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor lanced the infected boil and drained it very carefully.  医生刺破感染的脓肿，并十分小心地把它排空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gore</td><td>/ˈɡɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（用角）刺伤，戳伤</td><td>gored by a bull<br>被公牛顶伤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farmer was gored by his own bull last spring.  这位农夫去年春天被自家的公牛顶伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harpoon</td><td>/hɑrˈpuːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>用鱼叉刺；鱼叉</td><td>harpoon a large whale<br>用鱼叉刺大鲸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crew harpooned the whale from a small boat.  船员们从小艇上用鱼叉刺中了那头鲸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jab</td><td>/ˈdʒæb/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>猛刺，戳</td><td>jab a needle into it<br>把针戳进去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse jabbed the needle in before I could look.  我还没来得及看，护士就把针刺了进去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pierce</td><td>/ˈpɪrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>刺穿，穿透</td><td>pierce the thick leather<br>刺穿厚皮革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The needle pierced the thick leather without much effort.  针没费多大力气就刺穿了厚皮革。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 puncture</td><td>/ˈpʌŋktʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>刺破，戳孔</td><td>puncture a tire<br>把轮胎刺破</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rusty nail punctured the front tire of my bicycle.  一根锈钉刺破了我自行车的前胎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spike</td><td>/ˈspaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>激起，使猛增</td><td>spike public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary spiked public interest in the region's vanishing wildlife.  这部纪录片激起公众对该地区濒危野生动物的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stab</td><td>/ˈstæb/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺，戳</td><td>stab it with a fork<br>用叉子戳它</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stabbed the map with his finger to show the place.  他用手指戳着地图指出那个地方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stick</td><td>/ˈstɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>坚持，不改口；粘住</td><td>stick to his story<br>坚持他的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under hours of questioning the witness stuck to her original account.  在数小时的盘问下，证人仍坚持她最初的说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lax</b></td><td>/læks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松懈的，不严格的；漫不经心的</td><td>lax security standards<br>松懈的安保标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lax security standards at the warehouse allowed the theft to go unnoticed.  仓库松懈的安保标准使这起盗窃未被发觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flabby</td><td>/ˈflæbi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>疲软的，无力的</td><td>flabby arms<br>松软无力的手臂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months without exercise left his arms flabby and weak.  几个月不锻炼使他的手臂松软无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flaccid</td><td>/ˈflæsɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不结实的，松弛的</td><td>flaccid muscles<br>松弛的肌肉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks in bed left his leg muscles flaccid and weak.  数周卧床使他的腿部肌肉松弛无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insecure</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪˈkjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不牢固的，不稳固的；缺乏自信的</td><td>an insecure ladder<br>一把不牢固的梯子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shelf was so insecure that it collapsed under the books.  那个架子太不牢固，被书压塌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loose</td><td>/ˈluːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>释放，放开；松的</td><td>loose the dogs<br>放开猎狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The keeper loosed the hawk and watched it climb into the sky.  饲养员放开那只鹰，看它飞向天空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loosened</td><td>/ˈluːsənd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>松开的，放松的</td><td>a loosened knot<br>松开的绳结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He walked in with a loosened tie and tired eyes.  他领带松着、眼神疲惫地走进来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relaxed</td><td>/rɪˈlækst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>松弛的，放松的</td><td>a relaxed grip<br>放松的握力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her shoulders finally looked relaxed after the massage.  按摩后她的肩膀终于显得松弛了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slackened</td><td>/ˈslækənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松弛的，放松的</td><td>a slackened rope<br>松弛的绳子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The slackened sail flapped uselessly in the calm.  松垂的帆在无风中徒然拍动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsecured</td><td>/ˌʌnsɪˈkjʊrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未固定的，松脱的</td><td>an unsecured load<br>未固定的货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unsecured ladder slid off the roof of the van.  一把未固定的梯子从货车顶上滑落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derelict</td><td>/ˈderəlɪkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>玩忽职守的，不认真的</td><td>derelict in his duty<br>玩忽职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard was found derelict in his duty on the night of the theft.  那名警卫被认定在失窃当夜玩忽职守。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disregardful</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑrdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不顾的，漠视的</td><td>disregardful of safety<br>漠视安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The driver was disregardful of every posted speed limit.  这名司机漠视每一处限速标志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lazy</td><td>/ˈleɪzi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>懒惰的</td><td>too lazy to cook<br>懒得做饭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was too lazy to walk the dog on cold mornings.  寒冷的早晨他懒得去遛狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglectful</td><td>/nɪˈɡlektfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>neglectful of his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report found the agency neglectful of basic safety inspections.  报告认定该机构疏忽了基本安全检查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglecting</td><td>/nɪˈɡlektɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>忽视的，怠慢的</td><td>neglecting his studies<br>忽视学业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the term gaming, neglecting every assignment he received.  他整学期打游戏，忽视了收到的每项作业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 negligent</td><td>/ˈneɡlɪdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽大意的</td><td>negligent in his duties<br>对职责疏忽大意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company was found negligent in maintaining its equipment.  该公司被认定在设备维护上存在疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remiss</td><td>/rɪˈmɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>be remiss in one's duties<br>疏于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I would be remiss if I did not thank the volunteers.  如果我不感谢志愿者，那就是我的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slack</td><td>/slæk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>松弛的；疏忽的，大意的</td><td>slack about deadlines<br>对截止日期松懈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager was slack about deadlines and lost several clients.  新经理对截止日期很松懈，因此丢了好几个客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taut</td><td>/tɔːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧绷的</td><td>pull the rope taut<br>把绳子拉紧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wire was so taut that it hummed in the wind.  这根线绷得很紧，在风中嗡嗡作响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tense</td><td>/ˈtens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>紧绷的，紧张的</td><td>tense shoulders<br>紧绷的肩膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His tense jaw showed how hard he was concentrating.  他紧绷的下颌显示出他多么专注。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tight</td><td>/ˈtaɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的；紧的</td><td>tight with money<br>在钱上很抠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her uncle was famously tight with money all his life.  她舅舅一生在钱上出名地抠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attentive</td><td>/əˈtentɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，专注的；周到的</td><td>an attentive audience<br>专注的听众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse stayed attentive to every change in his breathing.  护士留心他呼吸的每一点变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careful</td><td>/ˈkerfəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，仔细的</td><td>careful consideration<br>慎重考虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be careful when you cross this busy street at night.  夜里穿过这条繁忙的街道时要小心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cautious</td><td>/ˈkɔːʃəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谨慎的，小心的</td><td>a cautious approach<br>谨慎的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investors remained cautious until the election results were announced.  在选举结果公布前投资者一直很谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conscientious</td><td>/ˌkɑːnʃiˈenʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>仔细的，一丝不苟的；有良心的，正直的</td><td>a conscientious worker<br>一位认真的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A conscientious editor checks every fact before the article goes to print.  一丝不苟的编辑会在文章付印前核实每一个事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 heedful</del></td><td>/ˈhiːdfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>heedful of the warning<br>留意警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heedful of the forecast, the climbers turned back early.  留意到天气预报，登山者提早返回了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mindful</td><td>/ˈmaɪndfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>留心的，注意的</td><td>mindful of the risks<br>留意风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Be mindful of the time when you answer each question.  回答每个问题时要留意时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vigilant</td><td>/ˈvɪdʒɪlənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警醒的，警惕的</td><td>remain vigilant<br>保持警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guards must remain vigilant throughout the long and quiet night shift.  守卫在漫长安静的夜班中必须始终保持警惕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wary</td><td>/ˈweri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>警惕的，谨慎的</td><td>wary of strangers<br>对陌生人警惕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children are taught to be wary of strangers offering free rides.  孩子们被教导要警惕主动提供搭车的陌生人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>license</b></td><td>/ˈlaɪsns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>许可，认可；自由</td><td>a license to practice<br>执业许可证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally obtained a license to practice medicine in three different states.  她终于取得了在三个不同州行医的执业许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allowance</td><td>/əˈlaʊəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>限额；津贴</td><td>a baggage allowance<br>行李限额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Each passenger has a baggage allowance of two small bags.  每位乘客的行李限额是两个小包。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authorization</td><td>/ˌɔːθərəˈzeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>授权，批准</td><td>official authorization<br>正式授权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one may enter the lab without written authorization.  未经书面授权，任何人不得进入实验室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clearance</td><td>/ˈklɪrəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>批准，许可</td><td>security clearance for staff<br>员工的安全许可</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot waited for clearance before entering the runway.  飞行员在进入跑道前等待许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concurrence</td><td>/kənˈkɜːrəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>同意，一致；同时发生</td><td>with the concurrence of<br>在…同意之下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan proceeded with the concurrence of all board members.  该计划在全体董事同意之下推进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consent</td><td>/kənˈsent/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>give one's consent<br>表示同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children cannot travel without their parents' consent.  没有父母的同意，孩子们不能出行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 empowerment</td><td>/ɪmˈpaʊərmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>授权，赋予权力</td><td>the empowerment of local councils<br>对地方议会的授权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform aimed at real empowerment of village committees.  这项改革旨在真正赋权给村委会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 granting</td><td>/ˈɡræntɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>准予，授予</td><td>the granting of permission<br>许可的授予</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The granting of the visa took nearly three months.  签证的批准花了将近三个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 warrant</td><td>/ˈwɔːrənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保证，担保；使有正当理由</td><td>warrant the quality<br>保证质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory warrants the quality of every tool it sells abroad.  这家工厂为其出口的每件工具的质量提供保证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 freedom</td><td>/ˈfriːdəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>自由，自主权</td><td>freedom of choice<br>选择的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Staff enjoy real freedom in setting their own hours.  员工在安排工作时间上有真正的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 latitude</td><td>/ˈlætɪtuːd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（行动或言论）自由</td><td>allow considerable latitude<br>给予相当大的自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students are allowed considerable latitude in choosing their own elective courses.  学生在选择选修课时被给予相当大的自由度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liberty</td><td>/ˈlɪbərti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自由；自主权</td><td>personal liberty<br>人身自由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ruled that the law restricted individual liberty unlawfully.  法院裁定该法非法限制了个人自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interdiction</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈdɪkʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，阻断，封锁</td><td>drug interdiction efforts<br>禁毒阻断行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Naval interdiction stopped most of the smuggling along the coast.  海军的阻断行动制止了沿岸大部分走私。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prohibition</td><td>/ˌproʊəˈbɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，禁令</td><td>a prohibition on smoking<br>禁止吸烟的规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city enforced a prohibition against building near the lake.  该市执行了禁止在湖边建房的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 proscription</td><td>/proʊˈskrɪpʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>禁止，查禁；剥夺权利</td><td>the proscription of books<br>对书籍的查禁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wartime proscription of certain newspapers lasted for several years.  战时对某些报纸的查禁持续了数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confinement</td><td>/kənˈfaɪnmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>限制，禁闭；监禁</td><td>solitary confinement<br>单独禁闭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of confinement indoors had left the children pale.  数月的室内禁闭让孩子们脸色发白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 custody</td><td>/ˈkʌstədi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拘留，监管</td><td>held in police custody<br>被警方拘押</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The suspect remained in custody until the hearing.  嫌疑人一直被拘押至听证会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imprisonment</td><td>/ɪmˈprɪzənmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>监禁，关押</td><td>life imprisonment<br>终身监禁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He faced ten years of imprisonment for the fraud.  他因诈骗面临十年监禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>lionize</b></td><td>/ˈlaɪənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>追捧，把…捧为名人</td><td>lionize a young author<br>把年轻作家捧为名人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The media began to lionize the young author almost overnight.  媒体几乎一夜之间开始狂热吹捧这位年轻作家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 canonize</td><td>/ˈkænənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，封为圣徒</td><td>canonize a saint<br>封为圣徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church voted to canonize the nun a century after her death.  教会在这位修女去世百年后决定将她封为圣徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dignify</td><td>/ˈdɪɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使有尊严，使显庄重</td><td>dignify the ceremony<br>使典礼显得庄重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her quiet presence dignified the otherwise chaotic opening ceremony.  她安静的在场使原本混乱的开幕式显得庄重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elevate</td><td>/ˈelɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，提高；使兴奋</td><td>elevate the mind<br>提升心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good literature can elevate the mind and refine one's taste.  优秀的文学能提升心智、陶冶品味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ennoble</td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enshrine</td><td>/enˈʃraɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>奉为神圣，珍藏</td><td>enshrine a right<br>将权利奉为神圣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constitution enshrines the right to free speech.  宪法将言论自由的权利奉为神圣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ensky</del></td><td>/ɪnˈskaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使升天，奉上天界</td><td>ensky a hero<br>把英雄奉上天界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old poem enskies the fallen warriors as bright stars.  这首古诗把阵亡战士奉为明亮的星辰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthrone</td><td>/ɪnˈθroʊn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使登王位，拥立</td><td>enthrone the young prince<br>拥立年轻王子登位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobles enthroned the young prince before his tenth birthday.  贵族们在小王子十岁前拥立他登位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorify</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːrəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞美，美化</td><td>glorify war and violence<br>美化战争与暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics said the film glorified war instead of questioning it.  批评者说这部电影美化战争而非质疑它。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humiliate</td><td>/hjuːˈmɪlieɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>羞辱，使丧失尊严</td><td>humiliate him in public<br>当众羞辱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher never humiliated a student for asking a simple question.  这位老师从不因学生问简单的问题而羞辱他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ludicrous</b></td><td>/ˈluːdɪkrəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，可笑的</td><td>a ludicrous idea<br>可笑的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price they asked was simply ludicrous.  他们的要价简直可笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 absurd</td><td>/əbˈsɜːrd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合理的，荒谬的</td><td>an absurd suggestion<br>荒谬的建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It's absurd to expect results in a single week.  期望一周内出结果是荒谬的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comical</td><td>/ˈkɑːmɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滑稽的，好笑的</td><td>a comical expression<br>滑稽的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog wore a comical hat all afternoon.  这只狗整个下午都戴着一顶滑稽的帽子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derisive</td><td>/dɪˈraɪsɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>嘲笑的，轻蔑的</td><td>derisive laughter from behind<br>背后的嘲笑声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A derisive shout came from the back of the hall.  大厅后面传来一声嘲笑的叫喊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derisory</td><td>/dɪˈraɪsəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐可笑的；微不足道的</td><td>a derisory pay offer<br>少得可笑的加薪方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company made a derisory offer of two percent.  公司提出了少得可笑的百分之二涨幅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 farcical</td><td>/ˈfɑːrsəkəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐可笑的，滑稽的</td><td>a farcical trial<br>一场荒唐的审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting became farcical when nobody could find the agenda.  没人找得到议程，会议变得荒唐可笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 laughable</td><td>/ˈlæfəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可笑的，荒唐的</td><td>a laughable excuse<br>一个可笑的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their offer was so low that it seemed laughable.  他们的报价低得可笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ridiculous</td><td>/rɪˈdɪkjələs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极其可笑的，滑稽的</td><td>look absolutely ridiculous<br>看上去滑稽极了</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clown's ridiculous hat made the children laugh out loud.  小丑那顶滑稽的帽子逗得孩子们大笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pathetic</td><td>/pəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>差劲的；可怜的</td><td>a pathetic excuse<br>差劲的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their defense in the second half was frankly pathetic.  他们下半场的防守说实话很差劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preposterous</td><td>/prɪˈpɑːstərəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒谬的，极其反常的</td><td>a preposterous claim<br>荒唐的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The idea that he acted alone is preposterous.  说他独自行动的说法荒谬至极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 risible</td><td>/ˈrɪzəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可笑的，荒唐的</td><td>a risible excuse<br>可笑的借口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His defense of the policy was frankly risible.  他为该政策的辩护说实话相当可笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silly</td><td>/ˈsɪli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚蠢的，傻的</td><td>a silly mistake<br>愚蠢的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was silly to climb that tall ladder without help.  没人帮忙就爬那么高的梯子太傻了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 logical</td><td>/ˈlɑːdʒɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合乎逻辑的，合理的</td><td>a logical conclusion<br>合乎逻辑的结论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Selling the old machines was the logical next step.  卖掉旧机器是顺理成章的下一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rational</td><td>/ˈræʃnəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理性的，合理的</td><td>a rational decision<br>理性的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to make a rational decision instead of an emotional one.  试着做理性而非情绪化的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>luminous</b></td><td>/ˈluːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发光的，明亮的</td><td>a luminous dial<br>夜光表盘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luminous dial of his watch glowed faintly in the dark.  他手表的夜光表盘在黑暗中微微发亮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 astral</td><td>/ˈæstrəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>星的，星光般的</td><td>a faint astral glow<br>微弱的星光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An astral light hung above the quiet desert.  一片星光悬在寂静的沙漠上空。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 brilliant</td><td>/ˈbrɪljənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪颖的；辉煌的</td><td>a brilliant student<br>才华出众的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a brilliant mathematician long before she turned twenty.  她还没到二十岁就已是出色的数学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguished</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卓越的，杰出的；高贵的</td><td>a distinguished scholar<br>杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize went to a distinguished historian of medieval law.  奖项授予一位杰出的中世纪法律史学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illustrious</td><td>/ɪˈlʌstriəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，杰出的</td><td>an illustrious career<br>辉煌杰出的生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She retired after an illustrious career in medical research.  在医学研究领域度过杰出的生涯后，她退休了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 notable</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显著的；值得注意的</td><td>a notable achievement<br>显著的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has been a notable drop in traffic accidents this year.  今年交通事故有显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 noteworthy</td><td>/ˈnoʊˌtwɜːrði/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>值得注意的，显著的</td><td>a noteworthy achievement<br>一项值得注意的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The most noteworthy feature of the report is its honesty.  这份报告最值得注意的地方是它的诚实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preeminent</td><td>/priˈemɪnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>优秀的，重要的</td><td>the preeminent scholar<br>最杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is the preeminent authority on medieval trade routes in Europe.  他是研究欧洲中世纪贸易路线最杰出的权威。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prestigious</td><td>/preˈstɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有声望的，受尊敬的</td><td>a prestigious award<br>有声望的奖项</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He turned down a prestigious post to stay near his family.  他为了留在家人身边拒绝了一个有声望的职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redoubtable</td><td>/rɪˈdaʊtəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的，值得尊敬的；可怕的</td><td>a redoubtable rival<br>令人敬畏的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She faced a redoubtable rival in the final round of the tournament.  她在锦标赛决赛轮中遇上一位令人敬畏的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 signal</td><td>/ˈsɪɡnəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>（在重要性、成就方面）非同寻常的；给出信号</td><td>a signal achievement<br>非同寻常的成就</td></tr>
