<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most law-abiding citizens never see the inside of a courtroom.  大多数守法公民从未进过法庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 careless</td><td>/ˈkerləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>粗心的，疏忽的</td><td>a careless mistake<br>粗心的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark ruined months of patient negotiation.  一句不慎的话毁了数月的耐心谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inattentive</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈtentɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，漫不经心的</td><td>an inattentive student<br>不专心的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inattentive driver failed to see the red light.  这位漫不经心的司机没看到红灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unmindful</td><td>/ʌnˈmaɪndfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不留意的，未察觉的</td><td>unmindful of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unmindful of the time, they talked until well past midnight.  他们没在意时间，一直聊到深夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unthinking</td><td>/ʌnˈθɪŋkɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不体贴的，欠考虑的</td><td>an unthinking remark<br>欠考虑的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unthinking comment hurt her more than he realized.  他那句欠考虑的话伤她比他以为的更深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unwary</td><td>/ʌnˈweri/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不警惕的，粗心的</td><td>trap the unwary traveler<br>诱骗粗心的旅客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such websites are designed to trick the unwary buyer.  这类网站专门欺骗不加防范的买家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>obsolete</b></td><td>/ˌɑːbsəˈliːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，被淘汰的</td><td>become obsolete<br>变得过时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Once a marvel, the machine became obsolete within five years of its release.  这台机器曾是奇迹，上市五年内就被淘汰了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antiquated</td><td>/ˈæntɪkweɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老的，过时的</td><td>an antiquated system<br>过时的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The office still runs on an antiquated system built in the eighties.  办公室仍在使用八十年代建成的过时系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archaic</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，久远的</td><td>archaic legal language<br>古旧的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract still uses archaic terms that nobody understands.  这份合同仍在使用无人理解的古旧术语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dated</td><td>/ˈdeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的</td><td>a dated hairstyle<br>过时的发型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture looked hopelessly dated beside the building's new glass walls.  在大楼新的玻璃幕墙旁，这些家具显得过时至极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fossilized</td><td>/ˈfɑːsəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵化的，化石化的</td><td>fossilized attitudes<br>僵化的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The department's fossilized rules discouraged every new hire.  部门僵化的规定让每个新人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 medieval</td><td>/mɪˈdiːvəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>中世纪的；过时的</td><td>medieval attitudes toward women<br>对女性的陈腐观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company's leave policy feels frankly medieval today.  这家公司的休假制度如今显得实在过时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moribund</td><td>/ˈmɔːrɪbʌnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>垂死的，即将消亡的</td><td>a moribund industry<br>垂死的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap imports left the region's textile industry moribund within a single decade.  廉价进口品在十年内就让该地区的纺织业濒于消亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moth-eaten</td><td>/ˈmɔːθ ˌiːtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虫蛀的；陈旧过时的</td><td>a moth-eaten coat<br>被虫蛀的外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a moth-eaten sweater from his college days.  他穿着大学时代那件被虫蛀的毛衣来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outdated</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌdeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，陈旧的</td><td>outdated equipment<br>过时的设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their outdated software could not handle the new files.  他们过时的软件无法处理新文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outmoded</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈmoʊdɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，废弃的</td><td>outmoded ideas<br>过时的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory still relies on outmoded machinery from the nineteen sixties.  这家工厂仍在使用二十世纪六十年代的过时机器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 outworn</del></td><td>/ˌaʊtˈwɔrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧的，过时的</td><td>outworn traditions<br>过时的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quietly abandoned the outworn methods of her teachers.  她悄悄抛弃了老师们过时的方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prehistoric</td><td>/ˌpriːhɪˈstɔːrɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>史前的；极古老的</td><td>prehistoric cave paintings<br>史前洞穴壁画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists found prehistoric tools beside the dried riverbed.  考古学家在干涸的河床旁发现了史前工具。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rusty</td><td>/ˈrʌsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音生涩沙哑的</td><td>a rusty voice<br>生涩的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of silence his voice sounded rusty and thin.  多年不语后，他的声音听起来生涩而单薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contemporary</td><td>/kənˈtempəˌreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>当代的；同时代的</td><td>contemporary art<br>当代艺术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery mixes ancient sculpture with contemporary photography.  这家画廊将古代雕塑与当代摄影混合展出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 current</td><td>/ˈkɜːrənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>现在的，通行的；水流，电流</td><td>current practice<br>现行做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The current version of the software fixes several old bugs.  该软件的现行版本修复了若干旧漏洞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modern</td><td>/ˈmɑːdərn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>现代的，新式的</td><td>modern architecture<br>现代建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Modern phones can do far more than early computers could.  现代手机的功能远超早期的计算机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recent</td><td>/ˈriːsənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>最近的，近来的</td><td>in recent years<br>近几年来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Recent studies suggest that short naps improve memory.  最近的研究表明小睡能改善记忆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>onset</b></td><td>/ˈɑːnset/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起始，开始</td><td>the onset of winter<br>冬天的开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The onset of winter caught the expedition without adequate shelter or fuel.  冬天的到来让探险队没有足够的庇护所和燃料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alpha</td><td>/ˈælfə/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>开端，第一个；最强者</td><td>the alpha and omega<br>始与终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curiosity was the alpha of his entire scientific career.  好奇心是他整个科学生涯的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baseline</td><td>/ˈbeɪsˌlaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>基准，起点</td><td>establish a baseline<br>建立基准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recorded a baseline measurement before the treatment began.  医生在治疗开始前记录了基准数据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commencement</td><td>/kəˈmensmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开始，开端；毕业典礼</td><td>the commencement of talks<br>谈判的开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The commencement of construction was delayed by heavy spring rains.  施工的开始因春季大雨而延后。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dawn</td><td>/ˈdɔːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>开端；黎明</td><td>the dawn of civilization<br>文明的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Printing marked the dawn of mass education in Europe.  印刷术标志着欧洲大众教育的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genesis</td><td>/ˈdʒenəsɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>起源，创始</td><td>the genesis of the crisis<br>危机的起源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The genesis of the movement lies in a single student protest.  这场运动的起源是一次学生抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inception</td><td>/ɪnˈsepʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>开端，开始</td><td>since its inception<br>自创立以来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club has doubled in size since its inception three years ago.  这个俱乐部自三年前创立以来规模翻了一倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kickoff</td><td>/ˈkɪkˌɔːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>开始，开端</td><td>the kickoff of the campaign<br>运动的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kickoff of the fundraising drive drew hundreds of donors.  募捐活动的启动吸引了数百名捐赠者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nascence</del></td><td>/ˈneɪsəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诞生，发生，起源</td><td>the nascence of a movement<br>一场运动的诞生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Those pamphlets document the nascence of the labor movement.  那些小册子记录了工人运动的诞生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outset</td><td>/ˈaʊtset/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开端，开始</td><td>from the outset<br>从一开始</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the outset, she made it clear that she would not compromise.  从一开始，她就明确表示自己不会妥协。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 threshold</td><td>/ˈθreʃˌhoʊld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>开端；门槛，界限</td><td>on the threshold of change<br>处于变革的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country stands on the threshold of a major reform.  这个国家正处于一场重大改革的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclusion</td><td>/kənˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结束；结论</td><td>bring to a conclusion<br>使结束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial reached its conclusion after eleven long weeks.  审判在漫长的十一周后结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ending</td><td>/ˈendɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结局，结尾</td><td>a happy ending<br>幸福的结局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel has an ending that nobody sees coming.  这部小说的结局谁都想不到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 omega</td><td>/oʊˈmeɡə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结尾，最后</td><td>the alpha and omega<br>始与终</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For him, that discovery was the omega of a lifelong search.  对他而言，那个发现是毕生求索的终点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 termination</td><td>/tərməˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>the termination of the contract<br>合同的终止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Either side may request termination of the agreement early.  任何一方都可要求提前终止协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ordeal</b></td><td>/ɔːrˈdiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>严峻的考验</td><td>a terrible ordeal<br>可怕的折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Waiting for the test results was an ordeal she would never forget.  等待检测结果是她永远不会忘记的一场煎熬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calvary</td><td>/ˈkælvəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极度的苦难，磨难</td><td>a personal calvary<br>个人的苦难历程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her long illness became a calvary that lasted almost ten years.  她的久病成了一场近十年的苦难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crucible</td><td>/ˈkruːsəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>严峻考验；坩埚</td><td>the crucible of war<br>战争的严酷考验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign was a crucible that tested every young officer's nerve.  这场战役是考验每位年轻军官胆量的严峻磨炼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fire</td><td>/ˈfaɪər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>解雇；开火；火</td><td>fire an idle worker<br>解雇懒散的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager fired two clerks after the audit found losses.  审计查出亏损后经理解雇了两名职员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gauntlet</td><td>/ˈɡɔːntlət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>严峻考验；夹击的鞭打</td><td>run the gauntlet<br>受夹击般的考验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New employees must run the gauntlet of three tough interviews.  新员工必须经受三轮艰难面试的考验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enjoyment</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒɔɪmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>享受，乐趣</td><td>for sheer enjoyment<br>纯为享乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He reads old detective novels purely for his own enjoyment.  他读旧侦探小说纯粹为了自己的乐趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pleasure</td><td>/ˈpleʒər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>愉快，快乐</td><td>take pleasure in reading<br>以阅读为乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She takes great pleasure in tending her small garden.  她非常喜爱照料自己的小花园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ostensible</b></td><td>/ɑːˈstensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面上的，佯装的</td><td>the ostensible reason<br>表面上的理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ostensible purpose of the trip was research, but he went sightseeing.  这次旅行表面上的目的是研究，但他去观光了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apparent</td><td>/əˈperənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的；表面上的</td><td>for no apparent reason<br>没有明显原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It soon became apparent that the machine had never worked.  很快就明显看出这台机器从未运转过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assumed</td><td>/əˈsuːmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>假装的，假冒的</td><td>under an assumed name<br>用假名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He traveled under an assumed name for several years.  他用假名旅行了好几年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 evident</td><td>/ˈevɪdənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，显然的</td><td>clearly evident<br>显而易见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for music was evident from a very early age.  她的音乐才华从很小就显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ostensive</del></td><td>/ɑˈstɛnsɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明示的，直接指示的</td><td>an ostensive definition<br>明示定义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teachers often use ostensive gestures when explaining words to small children.  老师给幼儿讲词时常用明示的手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumed</td><td>/prəˈzuːmd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>推定的，假定的</td><td>the presumed cause<br>推定的原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Faulty wiring is still the presumed cause of the fire.  线路故障仍是这场火灾的推定原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 putative</td><td>/ˈpjuːtətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>推测的，假定的</td><td>the putative author<br>推定的作者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scholars question whether the putative author actually wrote the disputed play.  学者们怀疑那位推定的作者是否真写了这部有争议的剧本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reputed</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>据说的，号称的</td><td>the reputed owner<br>名义上的所有者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is the reputed owner of three houses on this street.  据说这条街上有三栋房子是他的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seeming</td><td>/ˈsiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面上的，貌似的</td><td>seeming indifference<br>表面上的冷淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her seeming calm hid a week of sleepless worry.  她表面的平静掩盖了一周的失眠焦虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supposed</td><td>/səˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>假定的，所谓的</td><td>the supposed benefits<br>所谓的好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody could measure the supposed benefits of the expensive program.  没人能衡量这个昂贵项目所谓的好处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prima facie</td><td>/ˌpraɪmə ˈfeɪʃi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>初步看来的，表面上的</td><td>prima facie evidence<br>初步证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The police say they have prima facie evidence linking him to the theft.  警方称有初步证据将他与盗窃案联系起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 actual</td><td>/ˈæktʃuəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，实际的</td><td>the actual cost<br>实际成本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actual cost was far higher than the original estimate.  实际成本远高于最初的估价。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 real</td><td>/ˈriːl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>真实的，真正的</td><td>a real event<br>真实事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film is based on a real event from 1943.  这部电影基于1943年的一件真实事件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>outgrowth</b></td><td>/ˈaʊtɡroʊθ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结果，后果</td><td>a natural outgrowth of<br>…的自然结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new festival was a natural outgrowth of the summer concerts.  这个新节庆是夏季音乐会的自然产物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aftereffect</td><td>/ˈæftərəˌfekt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>后果，后遗症</td><td>a lasting aftereffect<br>持久的后遗影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dizziness is a common aftereffect of this medication.  头晕是这种药常见的后遗反应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aftermath</td><td>/ˈæftərˌmæθ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>后果，余波，影响</td><td>in the aftermath of<br>在…的余波中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Aid arrived slowly in the aftermath of the flood.  洪水过后援助迟迟才到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequence</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsəkwens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结果；重要性，价值</td><td>of little consequence<br>无关紧要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The full consequences of the new policy will not be clear for years.  这项新政策的全部后果多年内都不会明朗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fate</td><td>/ˈfeɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>命运，宿命；注定</td><td>seal someone's fate<br>决定某人的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark sealed his political fate.  一句不慎的话决定了他的政治命运。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fruit</td><td>/ˈfruːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>成果，结果；水果</td><td>the fruit of his labor<br>他劳动的成果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This building is the fruit of ten years' planning.  这座楼是十年规划的成果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outcome</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌkʌm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>结果，结局</td><td>the likely outcome<br>可能的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nobody could predict the outcome of such a close election.  没人能预测这样势均力敌的选举结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 product</td><td>/ˈprɑːdəkt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>产物，结果；产品</td><td>a product of its time<br>时代的产物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The law was a product of decades of public pressure.  这项法律是数十年公众压力的产物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 result</td><td>/rɪˈzʌlt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>结果；导致，产生</td><td>as a result of<br>由于…的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay resulted from a simple error in the schedule.  这次延误源于日程上的一个小错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sequel</td><td>/ˈsiːkwəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>后续；结果；续篇</td><td>the sequel of the war<br>战争的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Widespread famine was the tragic sequel of the long war.  大范围的饥荒是这场长期战争的悲惨后果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sequence</td><td>/ˈsiːkwəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顺序，序列；接续的事物</td><td>in the right sequence<br>按正确顺序</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please perform the steps in the correct sequence.  请按正确顺序执行这些步骤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antecedent</td><td>/ˌæntəˈsiːdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>先前的；前事，前情</td><td>antecedent events<br>先前发生的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians study the antecedent causes of the revolution.  历史学家研究这场革命的先前起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cause</td><td>/ˈkɔːz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>原因；导致，引起</td><td>the root cause<br>根本原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain caused the small stream to flood the road.  大雨使这条小溪淹了公路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 occasion</td><td>/əˈkeɪʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>起因，诱因；场合</td><td>the occasion of the quarrel<br>争吵的起因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A trivial remark was the occasion of their long feud.  一句琐碎的话成了他们长期不和的起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>outmoded</b></td><td>/ˌaʊtˈmoʊdɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，废弃的</td><td>outmoded ideas<br>过时的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory still relies on outmoded machinery from the nineteen sixties.  这家工厂仍在使用二十世纪六十年代的过时机器。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antiquated</td><td>/ˈæntɪkweɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老的，过时的</td><td>an antiquated system<br>过时的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The office still runs on an antiquated system built in the eighties.  办公室仍在使用八十年代建成的过时系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archaic</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，久远的</td><td>archaic legal language<br>古旧的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract still uses archaic terms that nobody understands.  这份合同仍在使用无人理解的古旧术语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 demoded</del></td><td>/dɪˈmoʊdɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，不再流行的</td><td>demoded fashions<br>过时的时装</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His demoded suit drew smiles from the younger guests.  他过时的西装引来年轻客人的笑意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fossilized</td><td>/ˈfɑːsəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵化的，化石化的</td><td>fossilized attitudes<br>僵化的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The department's fossilized rules discouraged every new hire.  部门僵化的规定让每个新人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moribund</td><td>/ˈmɔːrɪbʌnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>垂死的，即将消亡的</td><td>a moribund industry<br>垂死的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap imports left the region's textile industry moribund within a single decade.  廉价进口品在十年内就让该地区的纺织业濒于消亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moth-eaten</td><td>/ˈmɔːθ ˌiːtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虫蛀的；陈旧过时的</td><td>a moth-eaten coat<br>被虫蛀的外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a moth-eaten sweater from his college days.  他穿着大学时代那件被虫蛀的毛衣来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obsolete</td><td>/ˌɑːbsəˈliːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，被淘汰的</td><td>become obsolete<br>变得过时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Once a marvel, the machine became obsolete within five years of its release.  这台机器曾是奇迹，上市五年内就被淘汰了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 outworn</del></td><td>/ˌaʊtˈwɔrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧的，过时的</td><td>outworn traditions<br>过时的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quietly abandoned the outworn methods of her teachers.  她悄悄抛弃了老师们过时的方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prehistoric</td><td>/ˌpriːhɪˈstɔːrɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>史前的；极古老的</td><td>prehistoric cave paintings<br>史前洞穴壁画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists found prehistoric tools beside the dried riverbed.  考古学家在干涸的河床旁发现了史前工具。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rusty</td><td>/ˈrʌsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音生涩沙哑的</td><td>a rusty voice<br>生涩的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of silence his voice sounded rusty and thin.  多年不语后，他的声音听起来生涩而单薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contemporary</td><td>/kənˈtempəˌreri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>当代的；同时代的</td><td>contemporary art<br>当代艺术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gallery mixes ancient sculpture with contemporary photography.  这家画廊将古代雕塑与当代摄影混合展出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 current</td><td>/ˈkɜːrənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>现在的，通行的；水流，电流</td><td>current practice<br>现行做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The current version of the software fixes several old bugs.  该软件的现行版本修复了若干旧漏洞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 modern</td><td>/ˈmɑːdərn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>现代的，新式的</td><td>modern architecture<br>现代建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Modern phones can do far more than early computers could.  现代手机的功能远超早期的计算机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>override</b></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈraɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>推翻；不顾；覆盖</td><td>override a veto<br>推翻否决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Congress voted to override the veto and pass the spending bill.  国会投票推翻否决，通过了该支出法案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 neglect</td><td>/nəˈɡlekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>忽视，疏忽；忽略，怠慢</td><td>neglect his duties<br>疏忽职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He neglected his duties for weeks and was finally dismissed.  他数周疏忽职责，最终被解雇了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overpass</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌpæs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>忽略，越过</td><td>overpass minor faults<br>忽略小的过失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor overpassed several errors in the final draft.  编辑忽略了终稿中的几处错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pass over</td><td>/pæs ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不予考虑，忽略</td><td>pass over a candidate<br>忽略某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee passed over three senior staff and promoted a newcomer.  委员会跳过三名资深员工，提拔了一位新人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comply</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>遵从，服从</td><td>comply with the rules<br>遵守规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All drivers must comply with the new speed limits.  所有司机都必须遵守新的限速规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 observe</td><td>/əbˈzɜːrv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守；观察</td><td>observe the law<br>遵守法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All visitors are expected to observe the museum's rules.  所有参观者都应遵守博物馆的规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>overshadow</b></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈʃædoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使相形失色；使蒙上阴影</td><td>overshadow her earlier work<br>使她早期作品相形失色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her later novels overshadow the modest work that first made her famous.  她后来的小说使那部最初让她成名的平实作品相形失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eclipse</td><td>/ɪˈklɪps/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使声望下降，使黯然失色</td><td>eclipse his earlier fame<br>使他早年的名声黯然</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His later failures eclipsed the fame he had earned as a young pianist.  他后来的失败使他年轻时作为钢琴家赢得的名声黯然失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outrank</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈræŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>级别高于，重要性超过</td><td>outrank a colleague<br>级别高于同事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A captain outranks a lieutenant in every branch of the service.  在各军种中上尉级别都高于中尉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outshine</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈʃaɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>比…更出色，使相形失色</td><td>outshine one's rivals<br>使对手相形失色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist outshone every other performer at the festival.  这位年轻小提琴手让音乐节上其他表演者相形失色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outstrip</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈstrɪp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>超过，胜过</td><td>outstrip demand<br>超过需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Demand for the vaccine quickly outstripped the factory's supply.  疫苗需求很快超过了工厂的供应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overbalance</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈbæləns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重要性超过；使失去平衡</td><td>overbalance the risks<br>重要性超过风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The costs of the plan overbalance any short-term gains.  该计划的成本超过了任何短期收益。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overweigh</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈweɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重要性超过；压倒</td><td>overweigh the objections<br>分量超过反对意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advantages of the merger overweigh the risks involved.  合并的好处超过了其中的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fall behind</td><td>/fɔl bɪˈhaɪnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>落后，跟不上</td><td>fall behind schedule<br>落后于计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell behind the rest of the class after missing two weeks.  缺课两周后他落后于全班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>overwhelm</b></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈwelm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>淹没</td><td>overwhelmed by work<br>被工作压得喘不过气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That spring the whole valley was overwhelmed by floods from melting snow.  那年春天，整个山谷被融雪形成的洪水淹没。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deluge</td><td>/ˈdeljuːdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大暴雨；大量</td><td>a deluge of complaints<br>大量投诉涌来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The announcement brought a deluge of angry letters to the editor.  这项公告引来大量愤怒的读者来信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drown</td><td>/ˈdraʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>溺死；淹没</td><td>drown out the noise<br>把噪音压过去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Loud applause drowned out the speaker's closing words completely.  响亮的掌声完全淹没了演讲者的结束语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 engulf</td><td>/ɪnˈɡʌlf/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吞没，包围</td><td>engulfed in flames<br>被火焰吞没</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fire quickly engulfed the abandoned warehouse by the river.  大火迅速吞没了河边的废弃仓库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inundate</td><td>/ˈɪnʌndeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>淹没</td><td>inundated with requests<br>被请求淹没</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers inundated the newspaper with letters after the controversial editorial appeared.  那篇有争议的社论刊出后，读者的来信淹没了报社。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overflow</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈfloʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>溢出，泛滥</td><td>overflow the river banks<br>河水漫过堤岸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Melting snow made the river overflow its banks last spring.  去年春天融雪使河水漫过堤岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submerge</td><td>/səbˈmɜːrdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使淹没</td><td>submerge the whole valley<br>淹没整个山谷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new dam will submerge the whole valley and three old villages.  新水坝将淹没整个山谷和三个古村。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 submerse</del></td><td>/səbˈmɜːrs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使浸没，淹没</td><td>submerse the roots in water<br>把根浸入水中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Gardeners submerse the bare roots in water before planting them.  园丁在栽种前把裸根浸入水中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swamp</td><td>/ˈswɑːmp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>困境；沼泽</td><td>a swamp of paperwork<br>文书工作的泥潭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was lost in a swamp of forms and old receipts.  他陷在表格和旧收据的泥潭里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 drain</td><td>/ˈdreɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耗尽，使枯竭</td><td>drain the country's resources<br>耗尽国家资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long war drained the country's resources and its morale.  漫长的战争耗尽了国家的资源和士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>overwrought</b></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈrɔːt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>十分激动的，不安的；过分华丽的</td><td>an overwrought mother<br>极度不安的母亲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The overwrought father paced the hospital corridor for hours.  那位极度不安的父亲在医院走廊来回踱步了几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agitated</td><td>/ˈædʒəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的，激动的</td><td>become visibly agitated<br>明显焦躁起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew agitated when the police refused to answer her questions.  警察拒绝回答她的问题时，她变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excited</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>兴奋的，激动的</td><td>excited about the trip<br>对旅行很兴奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were too excited to sleep before the trip.  孩子们太兴奋了，旅行前根本睡不着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frenzied</td><td>/ˈfrenzid/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>狂乱的，疯狂的</td><td>frenzied activity<br>狂乱的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traders made frenzied attempts to sell before the market closed.  交易员在收市前疯狂地尝试抛售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heated</td><td>/ˈhiːtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>激烈的，激动的</td><td>a heated argument<br>激烈的争论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A heated debate broke out over the proposed budget cuts.  就削减预算的提议爆发了激烈争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hectic</td><td>/ˈhektɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忙乱的，兴奋而混乱的</td><td>a hectic schedule<br>忙乱的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 December is always hectic for everyone working in retail.  对零售业者来说十二月总是忙乱不堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hyperactive</td><td>/ˌhaɪpərˈæktɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度活跃的，多动的</td><td>a hyperactive child<br>多动的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher struggled to calm a hyperactive class before the test.  老师考试前费力让过度活跃的班级安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overactive</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈæktɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度活跃的</td><td>an overactive imagination<br>过于活跃的想象力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His overactive imagination turned every shadow into a burglar.  他过度活跃的想象把每个影子都变成小偷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baroque</td><td>/bəˈroʊk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>装饰华丽的，过分雕琢的，复杂的</td><td>baroque ornamentation<br>华丽繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceiling was covered with heavy baroque ornamentation in gold.  天花板上覆满了华丽繁复的金色装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 bedizened</del></td><td>/bɪˈdaɪzənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>俗丽装饰的，过分打扮的</td><td>bedizened with jewels<br>珠宝堆砌装饰的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She appeared bedizened with cheap beads and bright ribbons.  她浑身俗丽地缀着廉价珠子和鲜艳丝带。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flamboyant</td><td>/flæmˈbɔɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>艳丽夺目的，炫耀的，充满装饰的</td><td>a flamboyant style<br>浮夸炫目的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The singer wore a flamboyant coat covered in mirrors and feathers.  歌手穿着一件缀满镜片和羽毛的艳丽夺目的外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 florid</td><td>/ˈflɔːrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辞藻华丽的，花哨的</td><td>florid prose<br>辞藻华丽的散文</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic mocked the florid prose of the young novelist.  评论家嘲讽这位年轻小说家辞藻华丽的文风。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fussy</td><td>/ˈfʌsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挑剔的，过分讲究的</td><td>fussy about small details<br>对小细节很挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old tailor is fussy about the smallest details.  这位老裁缝对最小的细节都很挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 gingerbreaded</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɪndʒərbrɛdɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过分装饰的，花哨的</td><td>a gingerbreaded facade<br>过分装饰的门面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gingerbreaded old house was covered with fussy wooden trim.  这座花哨的老房子布满繁琐的木饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ornate</td><td>/ɔrˈneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>华丽的，装饰繁复的</td><td>ornate decoration<br>繁复的装饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hall's ornate ceiling took two years to paint.  大厅华丽繁复的天花板画了两年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overdecorated</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈdɛkəreɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>装饰过度的</td><td>an overdecorated room<br>装饰过度的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The overdecorated ballroom felt more tiring than elegant.  装饰过度的舞厅让人觉得累而不是雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 collected</td><td>/kəˈlektəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，冷静的</td><td>calm and collected<br>冷静镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed cool and collected throughout the tense negotiation.  整个紧张的谈判过程中她都保持冷静镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 composed</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇静的，沉着的</td><td>remain composed<br>保持镇静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed composed while answering the reporters' hostile questions.  她在回答记者敌意的提问时始终保持镇静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 placid</td><td>/ˈplæsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的，淡定的</td><td>a placid temperament<br>沉静的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His placid temperament made him ideal for this very stressful job.  他沉静的性情使他非常适合这份高压工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 serene</td><td>/səˈriːn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安详的</td><td>a serene smile<br>安详的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She watched the serene surface of the lake at dawn without speaking.  她默默看着黎明时宁静的湖面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquil</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平静的，安宁的</td><td>a tranquil lake<br>平静的湖面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a tranquil afternoon reading beside the quiet river.  我们在静静的河边度过了一个安宁的下午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 austere</td><td>/ɔːˈstɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>朴素的，朴实无华的</td><td>an austere room<br>简朴的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He slept in an austere room with a bed and one chair.  他睡在一间只有一张床和一把椅子的朴素房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 severe</td><td>/səˈvɪr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的；严重的</td><td>a severe penalty<br>严厉的处罚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge imposed a severe penalty on the repeat offender.  法官对累犯施以严厉处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stark</td><td>/ˈstɑːrk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严酷的，荒凉的；鲜明的</td><td>a stark contrast<br>鲜明的对比</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photograph shows the stark landscape of the desert.  这张照片展现了沙漠荒凉的景观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unadorned</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈdɔːrnd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>朴素的，无装饰的</td><td>unadorned walls<br>朴素的墙面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chapel was unadorned except for one small wooden cross.  礼拜堂除一个小木十字外别无装饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>palaver</b></td><td>/pəˈlævər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>闲谈，废话；花言巧语</td><td>endless palaver about money<br>没完没了地谈钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After an hour of pointless palaver, the committee had decided nothing.  经过一小时无意义的闲谈，委员会什么也没决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 argument</td><td>/ˈɑːrɡjumənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（逻辑上的）论证，理由；争论</td><td>a convincing argument<br>有说服力的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He built a convincing argument for keeping the old bridge open.  他提出了一个令人信服的论证，主张保留旧桥通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 colloquy</td><td>/ˈkɑːləkwi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>正式谈话，会谈</td><td>a formal colloquy<br>正式会谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two ministers held a long colloquy behind closed doors.  两位部长闭门进行了长时间会谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conference</td><td>/ˈkɑːnfərəns/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>会议，商谈</td><td>hold a conference<br>举行会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union leaders requested a conference with the factory owners.  工会领袖要求与厂主会谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consult</td><td>/kənˈsʌlt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>请教，咨询；商议</td><td>consult an expert<br>咨询一位专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Before signing anything you should consult a lawyer you trust.  签任何东西前你都该咨询一位信得过的律师。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 council</td><td>/ˈkaʊnsəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>委员会，理事会</td><td>the city council<br>市议会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city council voted to build a new park near the river.  市议会投票决定在河边建一座新公园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counsel</td><td>/ˈkaʊnsəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>建议，劝告</td><td>counsel patience<br>劝人耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lawyer counseled him to say nothing to the press.  他的律师建议他对媒体什么都别说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debate</td><td>/dɪˈbeɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>辩论，争论；仔细考虑</td><td>a heated debate<br>激烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council debated the housing plan for three hours.  议会就住房计划辩论了三个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dialogue</td><td>/ˈdaɪəˌlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对话，对白</td><td>open a dialogue<br>开启对话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally opened a dialogue about wages.  双方终于就工资开启对话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parley</td><td>/ˈpɑːrli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>（与敌方的）谈判，会谈</td><td>a parley with the enemy<br>与敌方谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two generals agreed to parley before dawn.  两位将军同意在黎明前谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 babble</td><td>/ˈbæbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>发出含糊无意义的嘟囔声，胡乱说</td><td>babble on about nothing<br>没完没了地瞎扯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The toddler would babble happily for hours in his crib.  这个学步的孩子会在婴儿床里开心地咿呀乱说几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chatter</td><td>/ˈtʃætər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>喋喋不休，闲扯</td><td>chatter endlessly<br>喋喋不休地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children chattered endlessly during the long bus ride.  孩子们在漫长的巴士行程中喋喋不休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 converse</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>交谈，谈话</td><td>converse in French<br>用法语交谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two diplomats conversed quietly in French all evening.  两位外交官整晚都用法语轻声交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gabble</td><td>/ˈɡæbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>急促而含糊地说</td><td>gabble away nervously<br>紧张地急促乱说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child gabbled excitedly about everything he had seen that day.  孩子兴奋地急促讲述那天见到的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jabber</td><td>/ˈdʒæbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>快而不清楚地说</td><td>jabber away in Italian<br>用意大利语急促地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tourists jabbered away in Italian throughout the entire bus ride.  游客们在整段车程中都用意大利语喋喋不休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prate</td><td>/preɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闲聊，空谈</td><td>prate about nothing<br>空谈毫无内容之事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would prate for hours about matters he barely understood.  他会就自己几乎不懂的事情空谈好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prattle</td><td>/ˈprætəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>闲扯，唠叨</td><td>prattle on for hours<br>唠叨好几个小时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She prattled happily about her garden while we drank our tea.  我们喝茶时她开心地闲扯她的花园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rattle</td><td>/ˈrætəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>发出咔嗒声；使慌乱</td><td>rattle the windows<br>把窗户震得作响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passing train rattled every window in the old house.  驶过的火车震得老屋的每扇窗都咔咔作响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twitter</td><td>/ˈtwɪtər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>唧唧喳喳地说；鸟鸣</td><td>twitter with excitement<br>兴奋地喳喳说个不停</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guests twittered with excitement about the coming wedding.  客人们兴奋地喳喳谈论即将到来的婚礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>palpitation</b></td><td>/ˌpælpɪˈteɪʃn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>心悸，心跳加剧</td><td>heart palpitations<br>心悸，心跳加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient complained of palpitations whenever she climbed the stairs quickly.  病人抱怨每次快速上楼就会心跳加剧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beat</td><td>/ˈbiːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>打，击；击败</td><td>beat him mercilessly<br>无情地打他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards beat the prisoner until the officer finally intervened.  卫兵殴打囚犯，直到那位军官出面干预。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beating</td><td>/ˈbiːtɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（心脏）跳动；敲打</td><td>the beating of his heart<br>他的心跳</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could hear the frantic beating of her own heart.  她能听见自己心脏的狂跳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pulse</td><td>/ˈpʌls/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>脉搏，搏动</td><td>take someone's pulse<br>量某人脉搏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse felt his pulse and frowned slightly.  护士摸了他的脉，微微皱眉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 throb</td><td>/ˈθrɑːb/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>搏动，跳动</td><td>a dull throb<br>隐隐的搏动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A steady throb of pain ran through his injured knee.  一阵持续的搏动性疼痛穿过他受伤的膝盖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>panacea</b></td><td>/ˌpænəˈsiːə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>万能药，万灵药</td><td>a panacea for all ills<br>治百病的良药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Technology is useful, but it is no panacea for deep social problems.  技术有用，但它不是解决深层社会问题的万灵药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 catholicon</del></td><td>/kəˈθɑlɪkən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>万灵药，万应灵丹</td><td>a supposed catholicon<br>所谓的万灵药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free trade was hailed as a catholicon for every economic ill.  自由贸易被誉为治百病的万灵药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cure-all</td><td>/ˈkjʊr ɔl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>万能药，万灵丹</td><td>no simple cure-all<br>没有简单的万能药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Technology is not a cure-all for problems rooted in politics.  技术不是解决政治根源问题的万能药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elixir</td><td>/ɪˈlɪksər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>长生不老药，万能药</td><td>the elixir of life<br>长生不老药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alchemists spent lifetimes searching for an elixir of eternal youth.  炼金术士毕生寻找永葆青春的灵药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nostrum</td><td>/ˈnɑstrəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>秘方药；万灵丹</td><td>political nostrums<br>政治万灵方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters grew tired of stale nostrums offered every election year.  选民厌倦了每逢选举年推出的陈旧灵丹妙药。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>peculiarity</b></td><td>/pɪˌkjuːliˈærəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>独特之处；怪癖</td><td>a peculiarity of the dialect<br>该方言的一个特点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One peculiarity of the local dialect is its heavy use of double negatives.  这种当地方言的一个特点是大量使用双重否定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attribute</td><td>/ˈætrɪbjuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>特性，属性</td><td>an essential attribute<br>本质属性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is the most valuable attribute in a good teacher.  耐心是好老师最宝贵的特质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 character</td><td>/ˈkerɪktər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>特征，特色；性格</td><td>the character of the region<br>该地区的特色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Narrow streets give the old town its distinctive character.  狭窄的街道赋予老城独特的特色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 criterion</td><td>/kraɪˈtɪriən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>标准，准则</td><td>meet the main criterion<br>符合主要标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was the main criterion for choosing this particular supplier.  成本是选择这家供应商的主要标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diagnostic</td><td>/ˌdaɪəɡˈnɑːstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>特征性的；诊断的</td><td>a diagnostic feature<br>特征性的特点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The narrow leaves are a diagnostic feature of this species.  狭长的叶子是该物种的特征性特点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 differentia</del></td><td>/ˌdɪfəˈrɛnʃiə/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>区别性特征，种差</td><td>the essential differentia<br>本质的区别特征</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Logicians ask what differentia separates one species from another.  逻辑学家追问什么种差区分了两个物种。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feature</td><td>/ˈfiːtʃər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>想象，设想；以…为特色</td><td>hard to feature it<br>难以想象那情形</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I simply cannot feature him living quietly in the countryside.  我实在无法想象他在乡下安静生活的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fingerprint</td><td>/ˈfɪŋɡərˌprɪnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>独有特征；指纹</td><td>a chemical fingerprint<br>化学特征标记</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every volcano leaves a chemical fingerprint in the ice layers.  每座火山都在冰层中留下化学特征。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hallmark</td><td>/ˈhɔːlmɑːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>典型的特征</td><td>the hallmark of quality<br>品质的标志</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful craftsmanship is the hallmark of every piece she makes.  精心的手工是她每一件作品的典型特征。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mark</td><td>/ˈmɑːrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>记下，标记</td><td>mark the date<br>记下日期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She marked the passage with a thin pencil line.  她用细铅笔线标记了那段文字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 note</td><td>/ˈnoʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>指出，提到；注意</td><td>note that the data differ<br>指出数据有差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge noted that both accounts were plainly inconsistent.  法官指出两份陈述明显互相矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 particularity</td><td>/pərˌtɪkjəˈlærəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>特性，独特之处</td><td>the particularity of each case<br>每个案例的特殊性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The essay ignores the particularity of each local tradition.  这篇文章忽视了各地传统的独特之处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quality</td><td>/ˈkwɑːləti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>品质，水平；特性</td><td>work of high quality<br>高水平的作品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judges praised the quality of her early work.  评委称赞了她早期作品的水准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 specific</td><td>/spəˈsɪfɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，特定的；专门的</td><td>a specific example<br>一个具体的例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Can you give me a specific example of the problem?  你能给我一个这个问题的具体例子吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trait</td><td>/ˈtreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>特点，特征</td><td>an inherited trait<br>遗传特征</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curiosity is a trait shared by nearly all great scientists.  好奇心是几乎所有伟大科学家共有的特征。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pedagogical</b></td><td>/ˌpedəˈɡɑːdʒɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>教学的；教师的</td><td>pedagogical methods<br>教学方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new textbook reflects modern pedagogical methods and lively examples.  这本新教材体现了现代教学方法和生动的例子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 preceptorial</del></td><td>/ˌprisɛpˈtɔriəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>导师的，导师指导的</td><td>preceptorial teaching<br>导师制教学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The college relies on small preceptorial groups rather than large lectures.  这所学院靠小型导师小组而非大课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pupillary</td><td>/ˈpjupəlɛri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>学生的；瞳孔的</td><td>pupillary duties<br>学生的职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old statutes still describe pupillary duties in Latin.  古老章程仍用拉丁文描述学生的职责。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>peel</b></td><td>/piːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>脱去（衣服等）</td><td>peel off one's coat<br>脱下外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He peeled off his wet coat and hung it near the fire.  他脱下湿外套，挂在火边。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doff</td><td>/dɑːf/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>脱下</td><td>doff one's hat<br>脱帽致意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors were expected to doff their hats upon entering the small chapel.  参观者进入这座小礼拜堂时须脱帽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 douse</td><td>/daʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扑灭（火或灯）；浇透</td><td>douse the flames<br>扑灭火焰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Firefighters worked all night to douse the flames on the upper floors.  消防员整夜奋战，扑灭了高层的火焰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 put off</td><td>/pʊt ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>推迟，拖延</td><td>put off the meeting<br>推迟会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't put off the dentist appointment any longer than necessary.  牙医预约别再往后拖了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrug off</td><td>/ʃrʌɡ ˈɔf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>对…不予理会，一笑置之</td><td>shrug off the criticism<br>对批评一笑置之</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach shrugged off questions about his uncertain future.  教练对有关他前途未定的问题不予理会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 take off</td><td>/teɪk ˈɔf/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>脱掉；起飞</td><td>take off your coat<br>脱掉外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please take off your muddy boots before entering the house.  进屋前请脱掉沾泥的靴子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 don</td><td>/dɑːn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>穿上</td><td>don a uniform<br>穿上制服</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firefighters donned their heavy gear in less than one minute.  消防员不到一分钟就穿上了沉重的装备。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 put on</td><td>/pʊt ˈɑn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>穿上，戴上</td><td>put on a jacket<br>穿上夹克</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She put on her gloves before stepping into the snow.  她走进雪地前戴上了手套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perceptive</b></td><td>/pərˈseptɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的</td><td>a perceptive observation<br>敏锐的观察</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her perceptive comments revealed how carefully she had listened.  她敏锐的评论显示出她听得多么仔细。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fine</td><td>/ˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，精细的</td><td>a fine distinction<br>细微的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drew a fine distinction between courage and recklessness.  他在勇敢与莽撞之间划出了细微的区别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keen</td><td>/ˈkiːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的，锐利的</td><td>a keen eye for detail<br>对细节的敏锐眼光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographer has a keen eye for unusual light.  这位摄影师对特别的光线有敏锐的眼力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quick</td><td>/ˈkwɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>快的，迅速的</td><td>a quick decision<br>迅速的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quick decision saved the patient's life that night.  一个迅速的决定在那晚救了病人的命。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sharp</td><td>/ˈʃɑːrp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；锋利的；敏锐的</td><td>a sharp tongue<br>说话尖刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic has a sharp tongue but an unusually fair mind.  这位评论家言辞尖刻，心思却格外公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dead</td><td>/ˈded/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>死的；毫无生气的</td><td>a dead town at night<br>夜里死寂的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The resort is completely dead in the winter months.  这个度假地在冬季几个月里毫无生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 imperceptive</del></td><td>/ˌɪmpərˈsɛptɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感觉迟钝的，不敏锐的</td><td>an imperceptive reader<br>不敏锐的读者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only an imperceptive critic would miss the irony in her novel.  只有不敏锐的评论者才会错过她小说中的讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insensitive</td><td>/ˌɪnˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>感觉迟钝的，无感觉的</td><td>insensitive to pain<br>对疼痛无感觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scarred skin was completely insensitive to heat or pain.  那块疤痕皮肤对热和疼痛完全没有感觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 numb</td><td>/nʌm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>麻木的，无感情的</td><td>numb with grief<br>因悲伤而麻木</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Numb with grief, she answered the reporters' questions in a flat voice.  她因悲伤而麻木，用平板的声音回答记者的提问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perilous</b></td><td>/ˈperələs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>危险的</td><td>a perilous journey<br>危险的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The refugees made a perilous crossing in a small wooden boat.  难民们乘一条小木船进行了危险的横渡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dangerous</td><td>/ˈdeɪndʒərəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>危险的</td><td>a dangerous situation<br>危险的处境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Driving on that icy mountain road is extremely dangerous at night.  夜间在那条结冰山路上开车极其危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grave</td><td>/ˈɡreɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>严肃的，严重的；坟墓</td><td>a grave expression<br>严肃的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor's grave tone told us the news was bad.  医生严肃的语气告诉我们消息不好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grievous</td><td>/ˈɡriːvəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严重的，令人痛苦的</td><td>a grievous mistake<br>严重的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report described grievous failures in patient care.  报告描述了病人护理中的严重失误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hazardous</td><td>/ˈhæzərdəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有危险的，有害的</td><td>hazardous waste<br>有害废物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers must wear masks when handling hazardous chemicals.  处理有害化学品时工人必须戴口罩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 Jeopardizing</td><td>/ˈdʒepərˌdaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>危及的，使受损害的</td><td>jeopardizing public safety<br>危及公共安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gas leak was jeopardizing the safety of everyone inside.  煤气泄漏正危及楼内所有人的安全。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 menacing</td><td>/ˈmenəsɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威胁的，凶险的</td><td>a menacing look<br>凶险的眼神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two menacing dogs stood at the end of the narrow lane.  两条凶恶的狗站在窄巷尽头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 risky</td><td>/ˈrɪski/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冒险的，有风险的</td><td>a risky investment<br>有风险的投资</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Starting a restaurant during a recession seemed far too risky.  在经济衰退期开餐馆看来太冒险了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 serious</td><td>/ˈsɪriəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的，认真的；严重的</td><td>a serious student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is serious about becoming a research scientist.  她认真地想成为一名研究科学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 threatening</td><td>/ˈθretənɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威胁的；（天气）要变坏的</td><td>a threatening gesture<br>威胁性的手势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He made a threatening gesture and then walked quickly away.  他做了个威胁的手势，然后快步走开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 venturesome</del></td><td>/ˈventʃərsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>大胆冒险的；有风险的</td><td>a venturesome explorer<br>大胆的探险者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The venturesome sailor crossed the Atlantic in a tiny boat.  这位大胆的水手用小船横渡了大西洋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 harmless</td><td>/ˈhɑːrmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，不会造成伤害的</td><td>a harmless little joke<br>无伤大雅的玩笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The snake looks frightening but is completely harmless to humans.  这条蛇看起来可怕，但对人完全无害。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocent</td><td>/ˈɪnəsənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的，无恶意的；无辜的</td><td>an innocent remark<br>无恶意的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What began as an innocent joke ended in a serious quarrel.  一句本无恶意的玩笑最后引发了激烈争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innocuous</td><td>/ɪˈnɑːkjuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无害的；不会引起敌意的</td><td>an innocuous question<br>一个无恶意的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What seemed an innocuous question turned out to reveal her deepest secret.  看似无恶意的问题最终却揭露了她最深的秘密。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 safe</td><td>/ˈseɪf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>安全的，可靠的</td><td>a safe choice<br>稳妥的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Taking the train is the safe choice in this weather.  这种天气坐火车是稳妥的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unthreatening</td><td>/ˌʌnˈθrɛtənɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不具威胁的，温和的</td><td>an unthreatening manner<br>毫无威胁感的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unthreatening manner made the children relax at once.  她毫无威胁感的态度让孩子们立刻放松了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>perquisite</b></td><td>/ˈpɜːrkwɪzɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>额外补贴；职位特权</td><td>perquisites of office<br>职位的额外好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A company car was one perquisite of his new position.  一辆公司用车是他新职位的一项额外好处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cumshaw</del></td><td>/ˈkʌmʃɔ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小费，赏钱</td><td>a small cumshaw<br>一点小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dock workers expected a cumshaw for loading the crates quickly.  码头工人期待因快速装箱得到赏钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dividend</td><td>/ˈdɪvɪˌdend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>红利；额外的好处</td><td>pay a dividend<br>派发红利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Learning a second language paid an unexpected dividend in her career.  学第二语言给她的职业带来意外收益。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 donative</del></td><td>/ˈdoʊnətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>赠品，赏赐</td><td>a generous donative<br>丰厚的赏赐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The emperor's donative to the soldiers kept the army loyal.  皇帝给士兵的赏赐使军队保持忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extra</td><td>/ˈekstrə/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>额外的，多余的</td><td>an extra copy<br>多出的一份副本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We packed extra blankets in case the night turned cold.  我们多带了几条毯子，以防夜里变冷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gratuity</td><td>/ɡrəˈtuːɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小费；赏金</td><td>leave a gratuity<br>留下小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fifteen percent gratuity is already included in your bill.  百分之十五的小费已包含在账单中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gravy</td><td>/ˈɡreɪvi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>额外收入，意外之财</td><td>pure gravy<br>纯属外加的好处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any tips beyond his salary were simply gravy for him.  超出工资的小费对他纯属额外之财。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lagniappe</td><td>/ˈlænjæp/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>额外赠品，小礼物</td><td>a little lagniappe<br>一点额外赠品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The baker added a free roll as a lagniappe for regulars.  面包师给老顾客额外赠送一个免费面包。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perk</td><td>/ˈpɜːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>额外补贴，福利</td><td>a company perk<br>公司福利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free parking was the best perk of her new job.  免费停车是她新工作最好的福利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tip</td><td>/ˈtɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）倾斜，倾倒</td><td>tip the chair back<br>把椅子向后倾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not tip the glass or the tea will spill.  别把杯子倾斜，茶会洒出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>petulant</b></td><td>/ˈpetʃələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，爱发脾气的</td><td>a petulant remark<br>使性子的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor made a petulant remark and stormed off the stage.  那位演员使性子说了句话，然后气冲冲地离开了舞台。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 choleric</td><td>/ˈkɑːlərɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>a choleric temper<br>暴躁的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His choleric temper frightened every new employee in the office.  他暴躁的脾气让办公室里每个新员工都害怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouchy</td><td>/ˈɡraʊtʃi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，爱抱怨的</td><td>grouchy before coffee<br>喝咖啡前脾气差</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is grouchy every morning until the second cup of coffee.  每天早上第二杯咖啡之前他都很暴躁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grumpy</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmpi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，爱发牢骚的</td><td>a grumpy old neighbor<br>爱发牢骚的老邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our grumpy neighbor complains whenever the children play outside.  孩子们一在外面玩，爱发牢骚的邻居就抱怨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irascible</td><td>/ɪˈræsəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>an irascible old man<br>脾气暴躁的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The irascible coach shouted at the referee over every call.  这位暴躁的教练对每一次判罚都冲裁判大喊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 irritable</td><td>/ˈɪrətəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，急躁的</td><td>become irritable and tense<br>变得易怒紧张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lack of sleep makes him irritable by mid-afternoon.  睡眠不足使他到下午中段就变得易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peevish</td><td>/ˈpiːvɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，爱抱怨的</td><td>a peevish complaint<br>不耐烦的抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered in a peevish tone and refused to explain further.  她用不耐烦的语气回答，拒绝再解释。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perverse</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反常固执的，任性的</td><td>take perverse pleasure<br>反常地感到快意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He takes perverse pleasure in arguing against every popular opinion.  他反常地乐于反驳每一个流行观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pettish</del></td><td>/ˈpɛtɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，任性烦躁的</td><td>a pettish reply<br>烦躁任性的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a pettish reply and slammed the door.  她烦躁地回了一句，然后摔上了门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prickly</td><td>/ˈprɪkli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多刺的；棘手的</td><td>a prickly problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water rights remain a prickly problem for the two provinces.  水权对这两个省来说仍是个棘手问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 raspy</td><td>/ˈræspi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音刺耳的</td><td>a raspy voice<br>沙哑刺耳的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of smoking had left him with a low, raspy voice.  多年吸烟使他的嗓音低沉而刺耳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ratty</td><td>/ˈræti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，脾气坏的</td><td>get ratty with someone<br>对某人发脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gets ratty whenever he has not had enough sleep.  他睡不够就变得脾气很坏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stuffy</td><td>/ˈstʌfi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉闷乏味的，古板的</td><td>a stuffy lecture<br>沉闷的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stuffy lecture put half the audience to sleep.  那场沉闷的讲座让一半听众睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 testy</td><td>/ˈtesti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>a testy reply<br>不耐烦的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew testy when the reporters kept repeating the question.  记者反复问同一个问题时，他变得不耐烦起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forbearing</td><td>/fɔrˈbɛrɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，有耐心的</td><td>a forbearing teacher<br>宽容的老师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His forbearing manner calmed even the rudest customers.  他宽容的态度连最无礼的顾客都能安抚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obliging</td><td>/əˈblaɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐于助人的</td><td>an obliging neighbor<br>乐于帮忙的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An obliging neighbor watered our plants every day while we were away.  一位乐于帮忙的邻居在我们外出期间每天给植物浇水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patient</td><td>/ˈpeɪʃənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有耐心的，能忍耐的</td><td>patient with beginners<br>对新手有耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good teacher stays patient with beginners who ask the same question.  好老师对反复问同一问题的新手仍有耐心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stoic</td><td>/ˈstoʊɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>隐忍的，冷静的</td><td>stoic acceptance of pain<br>隐忍地承受痛苦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her stoic acceptance of pain impressed even the veteran nurses.  她隐忍地承受痛苦，连资深护士都为之动容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tolerant</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，容忍的</td><td>tolerant of criticism<br>能容忍批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good editor is tolerant of odd but honest writing.  好编辑对古怪却真诚的文字是宽容的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pioneer</b></td><td>/ˌpaɪəˈnɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>拓荒者，先驱者；开创，创造</td><td>a pioneer in genetics<br>遗传学的先驱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a pioneer in genetics long before the field became fashionable.  在遗传学成为热门之前很久，她就是这一领域的先驱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 colonist</td><td>/ˈkɑːlənɪst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>殖民者，开拓定居者</td><td>early colonists<br>早期殖民者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first colonists built rough shelters near the river mouth.  最早的殖民者在河口附近搭了简陋住所。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 colonizer</td><td>/ˈkɑːləˌnaɪzər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>殖民者，殖民开拓者</td><td>European colonizers<br>欧洲殖民者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The island's laws still reflect the habits of its colonizers.  该岛法律仍反映其殖民者的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homesteader</td><td>/ˈhoʊmstedər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>自耕农，宅地开拓者</td><td>prairie homesteaders<br>草原开拓农户</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Homesteaders plowed the dry plains hoping for a good harvest.  开拓农户耕作干旱的平原，盼望丰收。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 settler</td><td>/ˈsetlər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>定居者，移民</td><td>early settlers<br>早期定居者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Settlers cleared the forest and planted corn the first spring.  定居者伐林，在第一个春天种上玉米。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 begin</td><td>/bɪˈɡɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>开始，着手</td><td>begin work on the project<br>开始着手这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We will begin the renovation as soon as the permit arrives.  许可一到我们就开始装修。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 constitute</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，设立；构成</td><td>constitute a committee<br>设立委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board was properly constituted under the new rules.  依据新规则，董事会得以正式组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 establish</td><td>/ɪˈstæblɪʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>确证，证实；建立</td><td>establish the facts beyond doubt<br>确证事实无疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The investigation established the facts beyond any reasonable doubt.  调查毫无疑问地确证了事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inaugurate</td><td>/ɪˈnɔːɡjəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>开始</td><td>inaugurate a new era<br>开创一个新时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty inaugurated a long period of peace between the two nations.  这项条约开启了两国之间长期的和平。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 initiate</td><td>/ɪˈnɪʃieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>创始，发起</td><td>initiate a reform program<br>发起一项改革计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new governor plans to initiate a sweeping reform of rural education.  新州长计划发起一项农村教育的全面改革。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innovate</td><td>/ˈɪnəˌveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>创新，革新</td><td>innovate constantly<br>不断创新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small firms must innovate quickly or lose their customers.  小公司必须快速创新，否则会失去客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 institute</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪtuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>创立，制定；机构，学院</td><td>institute legal proceedings<br>提起法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school instituted a new dress code this term.  学校本学期制定了新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 introduce</td><td>/ˌɪntrəˈduːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引入，开始实行；介绍</td><td>introduce a new policy<br>推行一项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city introduced free bus travel for elderly residents.  该市开始为老年居民提供免费公交。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 launch</td><td>/ˈlɔːntʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>发起，开始；发射</td><td>launch a new campaign<br>发起新的活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to launch its cheapest model this autumn.  公司计划今秋推出其最便宜的型号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plant</td><td>/ˈplænt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>种植，栽种；植物；工厂</td><td>plant a row of trees<br>栽种一排树</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers came to plant young oaks along the muddy riverbank.  志愿者来到泥泞的河岸栽种小橡树。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 set up</td><td>/sɛt ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>建立，创办；安装，搭建</td><td>set up a company<br>创办一家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They set up a small company in the garage two years ago.  两年前他们在车库里创办了一家小公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 follower</td><td>/ˈfɑːloʊər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>追随者，拥护者</td><td>a loyal follower<br>忠实的追随者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained a devoted follower of his teacher for thirty years.  他三十年来一直是老师忠诚的追随者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 close</td><td>/kloʊz/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>关闭；结束</td><td>close the meeting<br>结束会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They closed the discussion before anyone could object.  他们在有人反对之前结束了讨论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 end</td><td>/ˈend/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>结束；末端</td><td>end the war<br>结束战争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike ended after both sides agreed on wages.  双方就工资达成一致后罢工结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shut</td><td>/ˈʃʌt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>关闭，关上</td><td>shut the door<br>关上门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please shut the windows before the storm arrives tonight.  今晚暴风雨来之前请关好窗户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terminate</td><td>/ˈtɜːrməˌneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>终止，结束</td><td>terminate the agreement<br>终止协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club terminated his membership without any explanation.  俱乐部未加解释就终止了他的会员资格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 phase out</td><td>/feɪz ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>逐步淘汰，分阶段停用</td><td>phase out old models<br>逐步淘汰旧型号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory will phase out diesel engines over five years.  工厂将在五年内逐步淘汰柴油发动机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pious</b></td><td>/ˈpaɪəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（信仰上）虔诚的</td><td>a pious old woman<br>虔诚的老妇人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pious old woman attended the early service every single morning.  那位虔诚的老妇人每天早上都参加早祷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devout</td><td>/dɪˈvaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虔诚的，笃信的</td><td>a devout believer<br>虔诚的信徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His devout aunt attended the little chapel every single morning.  他虔诚的姑妈每天早晨都去那间小教堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 godly</td><td>/ˈɡɑːdli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>虔诚的，敬神的</td><td>a godly life<br>虔诚的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was raised in a quiet, godly household near the river.  他在河边一个安静虔诚的家庭长大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 religious</td><td>/rɪˈlɪdʒəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虔诚的，宗教的</td><td>religious devotion<br>宗教上的虔诚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her religious upbringing shaped every choice she made.  她的宗教家教影响了她的每个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sainted</td><td>/ˈseɪntɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>圣洁的，德行高尚的</td><td>my sainted grandmother<br>我圣洁的祖母</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers still speak of the sainted doctor who never charged the poor.  村民仍谈起那位从不向穷人收费的圣洁医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saintly</td><td>/ˈseɪntli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>圣人般的，极为善良的</td><td>saintly patience<br>圣人般的耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her saintly patience with difficult patients impressed the whole ward.  她对难缠病人的圣人般耐心让整个病房折服。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 antireligious</del></td><td>/ˌæntirɪˈlɪdʒəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>反宗教的</td><td>antireligious propaganda<br>反宗教宣传</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime published antireligious pamphlets in every provincial town.  该政权在各省城印发反宗教小册子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 faithless</td><td>/ˈfeɪθləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不忠的，背信的</td><td>a faithless ally<br>不忠的盟友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king punished his faithless advisers without any mercy.  国王毫不留情地惩罚了背信的谋臣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 godless</td><td>/ˈɡɑːdləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不信神的，无神的</td><td>a godless age<br>不信神的时代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Preachers denounced the city as a godless place of pleasure.  传道者谴责这座城市是不信神的享乐之地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impious</td><td>/ɪmˈpaɪəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不敬神的</td><td>impious remarks<br>不敬神的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers considered his joke about the shrine deeply impious.  村民认为他拿神龛开的玩笑极为不敬神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irreligious</td><td>/ˌɪrɪˈlɪdʒəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不信教的，无宗教信仰的</td><td>an irreligious upbringing<br>无宗教的成长环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Though irreligious himself, he respected his wife's Sunday habits.  他自己不信教，却尊重妻子周日的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ungodly</td><td>/ənˈɡɑːdli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不敬神的，邪恶的</td><td>ungodly behavior<br>不敬神的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old preacher warned his flock against ungodly amusements.  老传道人告诫信众远离不敬神的娱乐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unholy</td><td>/ʌnˈhoʊli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不圣洁的，邪恶的</td><td>an unholy alliance<br>邪恶的联盟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the deal an unholy alliance of money and power.  批评者称这笔交易是金钱与权力的邪恶联盟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pitfall</b></td><td>/ˈpɪtfɔːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陷阱</td><td>avoid common pitfalls<br>避开常见陷阱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good guide will help beginners avoid the most common pitfalls.  好的指南能帮助新手避开最常见的陷阱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catch</td><td>/ˈkætʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>染上（疾病）；抓住</td><td>catch a cold<br>感冒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You will catch a cold if you go out with wet hair.  你要是湿着头发出门就会感冒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
