<tr><td>↳近 gimmick</td><td>/ˈɡɪmɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>花招，噱头</td><td>a marketing gimmick<br>营销花招</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free shipping was just a gimmick to attract buyers.  免运费只是吸引买家的花招。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gotcha</td><td>/ˈɡɑːtʃə/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>陷阱，坑人之处</td><td>a hidden gotcha<br>隐藏的坑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract has one gotcha buried in the fine print.  合同的小字里埋着一个坑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hitch</td><td>/ˈhɪtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>小障碍，故障</td><td>without a hitch<br>毫无阻碍地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony went off without a hitch.  典礼进行得毫无阻碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 joker</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>隐蔽的陷阱条款；爱开玩笑者</td><td>a joker in the contract<br>合同里的陷阱条款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A joker buried in the lease let the landlord raise rents.  租约中隐藏的陷阱条款让房东可以涨租。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snag</td><td>/ˈsnæɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>障碍</td><td>hit a snag<br>遇到障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The negotiations hit an unexpected snag over the question of wages.  谈判在工资问题上遇到意外障碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snare</td><td>/sner/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>陷阱，罗网；用陷阱捕捉</td><td>snare a rabbit<br>用套索捕兔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hunter set a snare near the stream to catch rabbits.  猎人在溪边设下套索捕兔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trap</td><td>/ˈtræp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>困住，使陷入；陷阱</td><td>trap him in a lie<br>使他陷入自己的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow trapped the climbers on the ridge for days.  大雪把登山者困在山脊上好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pivotal</b></td><td>/ˈpɪvətl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>最关键的，最为重要的</td><td>play a pivotal role<br>起关键作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her testimony played a pivotal role in the outcome of the trial.  她的证词对审判结果起了关键作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 critical</td><td>/ˈkrɪtɪkəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>关键的；批评的</td><td>a critical moment<br>关键时刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water supply is critical to the survival of this town.  供水对这座小镇的存续至关重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crucial</td><td>/ˈkruːʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常重要的，决定性的</td><td>a crucial decision<br>关键的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The next two weeks will be crucial to the project's survival.  接下来两周对这个项目的存亡至关重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decisive</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的；果断的</td><td>a decisive victory<br>决定性的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her testimony was decisive in the trial.  她的证词在审判中起了决定性作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 key</td><td>/ˈkiː/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>关键的；钥匙</td><td>a key factor<br>关键因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is the key element in any long working relationship.  信任是任何长期工作关系中的关键要素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsequential</td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːnsɪˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的，微不足道的</td><td>an inconsequential remark<br>无关紧要的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The difference between the two prices is quite inconsequential.  两个价格之间的差别微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsiderable</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的</td><td>an inconsiderable sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk is small but by no means inconsiderable.  风险虽小，却绝非微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insignificant</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的，微不足道的</td><td>a seemingly insignificant change<br>看似微小的改动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An insignificant error early on ruined the entire calculation.  早期一个微不足道的错误毁了整个计算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 marginal</td><td>/ˈmɑːrdʒɪnl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的</td><td>of marginal importance<br>无关紧要的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee dismissed the objection as marginal and moved on to weightier matters.  委员会认为这项反对无关紧要，转而讨论更重要的事情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 minor</td><td>/ˈmaɪnər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>较小的，不重要的</td><td>a minor problem<br>一个小问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only minor repairs were needed after the small accident.  小事故后只需要一些小修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 minute</td><td>/maɪˈnjuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极小的，微不足道的；细致的</td><td>a minute quantity<br>极小的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even a minute trace of the chemical can spoil the batch.  即使极微量的这种化学品也会毁掉整批产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nugatory</del></td><td>/ˈnuːɡətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关紧要的</td><td>a nugatory objection<br>无关紧要的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court dismissed his nugatory objection and proceeded with the hearing.  法庭驳回了他无关紧要的反对，继续进行听证。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trifling</td><td>/ˈtraɪflɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，不重要的</td><td>a trifling sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dismissed the missing coins as a trifling sum not worth reporting.  他把丢失的硬币当作微不足道的小钱，不值得上报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>placid</b></td><td>/ˈplæsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的，淡定的</td><td>a placid temperament<br>沉静的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His placid temperament made him ideal for this very stressful job.  他沉静的性情使他非常适合这份高压工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collected</td><td>/kəˈlektəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定的，冷静的</td><td>calm and collected<br>冷静镇定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed cool and collected throughout the tense negotiation.  整个紧张的谈判过程中她都保持冷静镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 composed</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇静的，沉着的</td><td>remain composed<br>保持镇静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She stayed composed while answering the reporters' hostile questions.  她在回答记者敌意的提问时始终保持镇静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coolheaded</del></td><td>/ˌkulˈhɛdɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷静的，沉着的</td><td>a coolheaded leader<br>冷静的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coolheaded pilot landed the damaged plane without panic.  冷静的飞行员毫不慌乱地降落了受损飞机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 level</td><td>/ˈlevəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>水平的，平的</td><td>on level ground<br>在平地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pitch the tent on level ground away from the stream.  把帐篷搭在远离溪水的平地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 limpid</td><td>/ˈlɪmpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>透明清澈的</td><td>a limpid mountain stream<br>清澈的山间溪流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The limpid water let us count stones on the bottom.  清澈的水让我们能数清水底的石头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 possessed</td><td>/pəˈzest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>发狂的，像被鬼附身的</td><td>fight like one possessed<br>像发狂似地搏斗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She played the final movement like someone possessed by the music.  她演奏末乐章时像被音乐附身一般狂热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recollected</td><td>/ˌrekəˈlektɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>镇定自若的；回忆起的</td><td>a recollected manner<br>从容镇定的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He faced the hostile crowd with a recollected calm.  他以镇定的从容面对充满敌意的人群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sedate</td><td>/sɪˈdeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>淡定的，安静的</td><td>a sedate atmosphere<br>安静沉稳的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel keeps a sedate atmosphere that older guests clearly appreciate.  这家酒店保持着安静沉稳的气氛，年长客人显然很喜欢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undisturbed</td><td>/ˌʌndɪˈstɜːrbd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受扰乱的，平静的</td><td>remain undisturbed<br>保持不受打扰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The papers lay undisturbed on the desk for years.  这些文件在桌上多年未被动过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unperturbed</td><td>/ˌʌnpərˈtɜːrbd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉着的，不慌乱的</td><td>unperturbed by criticism<br>对批评不为所动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed unperturbed by the sudden change of plan.  她对计划突变显得毫不慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 agitated</td><td>/ˈædʒəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦躁不安的，激动的</td><td>become visibly agitated<br>明显焦躁起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew agitated when the police refused to answer her questions.  警察拒绝回答她的问题时，她变得焦躁不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 discomposed</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊzd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心慌的，不安的</td><td>look visibly discomposed<br>显得明显心慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The question left the speaker discomposed for a moment.  这个问题让演讲者一时慌了神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disturbed</td><td>/dɪˈstɜːrbd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不安的，受扰的</td><td>deeply disturbed by news<br>被消息深深扰乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was disturbed by the noise from the next room.  他被隔壁的噪音扰得不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flustered</td><td>/ˈflʌstərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慌乱的，手足无措的</td><td>get flustered easily<br>容易慌乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew flustered when the lights went out.  灯熄灭时她变得慌乱起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 perturbed</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrbd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>焦虑的，心烦意乱的</td><td>perturbed by the delay<br>因延误而心烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seemed perturbed by the letter she had just opened.  她刚打开的那封信似乎让她心烦意乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upset</td><td>/əpˈset/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>使心烦，使不安；打乱</td><td>upset the schedule<br>打乱日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the closure upset the staff more than management expected.  停业的消息比管理层预想的更让员工不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>playful</b></td><td>/ˈpleɪfl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱开玩笑的，好打闹的</td><td>a playful tone<br>玩闹的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her playful tone made it clear that she was only teasing him.  她玩闹的语气清楚表明她只是在逗他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antic</td><td>/ˈæntɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滑稽可笑的</td><td>antic behavior<br>滑稽的举动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His antic gestures on stage made the whole audience laugh.  他在台上滑稽可笑的动作让全场观众大笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 coltish</del></td><td>/ˈkoʊltɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱开玩笑的</td><td>coltish high spirits<br>顽皮的兴奋劲儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her coltish humor kept the whole office laughing all morning.  她爱开玩笑的性子让整个办公室笑了一上午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 elfish</del></td><td>/ˈelfɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽皮的，小精灵般的</td><td>an elfish grin<br>顽皮的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy gave an elfish grin and darted behind the curtain.  男孩顽皮一笑，窜到窗帘后面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fay</td><td>/ˈfeɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>仙子般的，顽皮的；仙子</td><td>a fay charm<br>精灵般的魅力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fay manner made the children believe in fairies again.  她精灵般的举止让孩子们重新相信仙子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 frisky</td><td>/ˈfrɪski/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>活泼欢腾的</td><td>a frisky puppy<br>一只活泼的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frisky colt raced across the field at dawn.  黎明时那匹活泼的小马驹在田野上奔跑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 frolicsome</del></td><td>/ˈfrɑlɪksəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>嬉戏的，爱玩闹的</td><td>a frolicsome puppy<br>爱闹的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frolicsome children chased each other around the garden.  那些爱闹的孩子在花园里互相追逐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 larky</del></td><td>/ˈlɑrki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>爱闹的，爱开玩笑的</td><td>in a larky mood<br>一副爱闹的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was in a larky mood and kept teasing everyone at dinner.  他兴致很高，晚饭时一直逗大家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rollicking</td><td>/ˈrɑːlɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢闹的，喧闹嬉戏的</td><td>a rollicking comedy<br>欢闹的喜剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent a rollicking evening singing old songs together.  他们一起唱老歌，度过了一个欢闹的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sportive</td><td>/ˈspɔrtɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>嬉戏的，爱玩闹的</td><td>a sportive puppy<br>爱嬉闹的小狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sportive dolphins followed the boat for almost an hour.  嬉戏的海豚跟着船游了将近一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 earnest</td><td>/ˈɜːrnɪst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃认真的</td><td>an earnest student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An earnest student stayed behind to ask about the summer reading list.  一名认真的学生留下来询问暑期阅读书目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grave</td><td>/ˈɡreɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>严肃的，严重的；坟墓</td><td>a grave expression<br>严肃的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctor's grave tone told us the news was bad.  医生严肃的语气告诉我们消息不好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 serious</td><td>/ˈsɪriəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的，认真的；严重的</td><td>a serious student<br>认真的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is serious about becoming a research scientist.  她认真地想成为一名研究科学家。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sober</td><td>/ˈsoʊbər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>严肃的；节制的</td><td>a sober assessment<br>冷静的评估</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee offered a sober assessment of the country's economic prospects.  委员会对国家经济前景作出冷静的评估。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 solemn</td><td>/ˈsɑːləm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>庄严的，郑重的</td><td>a solemn promise<br>郑重的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony was solemn and lasted almost two hours.  仪式庄严肃穆，持续了近两个小时。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 somber</td><td>/ˈsɑːmbər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的，不愉快的</td><td>a somber mood<br>阴郁的情绪</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plebeian</b></td><td>/pləˈbiːən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平民的，社会下层的</td><td>plebeian tastes in music<br>平民化的音乐趣味</td></tr>

<tr><td><del>↳近 baseborn</del></td><td>/ˈbeɪsbɔrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>出身卑微的</td><td>a baseborn servant<br>出身卑微的仆人</td></tr>

</tbody>

</tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite his lowborn origins, he rose to command the fleet.  尽管出身低微，他仍升任舰队指挥。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 lumpen</td><td>/ˈlʌmpən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>下层的，穷困无产的</td><td>the lumpen masses<br>下层民众</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prole</td><td>/proʊl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无产者，工人阶级成员</td><td>a common prole<br>普通无产者</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 proletarian</td><td>/ˌproʊləˈteriən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>无产阶级的；无产者</td><td>proletarian literature<br>无产阶级文学</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 unwashed</td><td>/ənˈwɑːʃt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平民大众的；未洗的</td><td>the great unwashed<br>平民大众</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her genteel accent betrayed an expensive and very traditional upbringing.  她文雅的口音透露出昂贵而传统的成长背景。</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 The horse moved with a noble grace across the field.  那匹马以高贵的优雅姿态穿过田野。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patrician</td><td>/pəˈtrɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贵族，名门望族</td><td>an old patrician family<br>古老的贵族家庭</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wellborn</td><td>/ˈwelbɔːrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>出身高贵的</td><td>a wellborn officer<br>出身高贵的军官</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td><b>plush</b></td><td>/plʌʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢华的，豪华的</td><td>a plush hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 deluxe</td><td>/dəˈlʌks/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>豪华的，高级的</td><td>a deluxe suite<br>豪华套房</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 luxurious</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奢侈的</td><td>a luxurious hotel suite<br>豪华的酒店套房</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They spent their anniversary in a luxurious hotel suite overlooking the harbor.  他们在一间俯瞰港口的豪华酒店套房里度过了结婚纪念日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opulent</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的；华丽的</td><td>an opulent hotel lobby<br>豪华的酒店大厅</td></tr>

<tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 silken</td><td>/ˈsɪlkən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔软光滑的，丝绸般的</td><td>silken hair<br>丝绸般的头发</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sumptuous</td><td>/ˈsʌmptʃuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>豪华的，奢侈的</td><td>a sumptuous banquet<br>一场豪华的宴会</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳反 ascetic</td><td>/əˈsetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>苦行的，禁欲的；苦行者</td><td>an ascetic lifestyle<br>苦修的生活方式</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 austere</td><td>/ɔːˈstɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>朴素的，朴实无华的</td><td>an austere room<br>简朴的房间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He slept in an austere room with a bed and one chair.  他睡在一间只有一张床和一把椅子的朴素房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spartan</td><td>/ˈspɑːrtn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>简朴的，节约的</td><td>spartan living conditions<br>简朴的生活条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks lived in spartan rooms with only a bed and a lamp.  僧侣们住在只有一床一灯的简朴房间里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>poach</b></td><td>/poʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>水煮</td><td>poach an egg<br>水煮一个蛋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He likes to poach an egg for breakfast every Sunday morning.  他喜欢每个周日早上水煮一个蛋当早餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coddle</td><td>/ˈkɑːdəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>娇养，溺爱</td><td>coddle a spoiled child<br>娇养被宠坏的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coaches should not coddle athletes who refuse to train hard.  教练不该娇养那些不肯苦练的运动员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 parboil</del></td><td>/ˈpɑːrˌbɔɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…煮至半熟，焯水</td><td>parboil the potatoes<br>把土豆焯至半熟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parboil the carrots for five minutes before roasting them.  烤之前先把胡萝卜焯水五分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 simmer</td><td>/ˈsɪmər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>用小火慢煮，微沸</td><td>simmer gently<br>小火慢煮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let the soup simmer for twenty minutes before adding salt.  加盐前让汤小火煮二十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stew</td><td>/ˈstuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>炖菜；杂糅之物；忧虑</td><td>a stew of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The building is a stew of styles from three different centuries.  这座建筑是三个不同世纪风格的杂糅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pointer</b></td><td>/ˈpɔɪntər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>忠告，建议</td><td>give someone a few pointers<br>给某人一些建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach gave the new players a few useful pointers before the game.  赛前教练给新球员提了几条有用的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advice</td><td>/ædˈvaɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>建议，忠告</td><td>take my advice<br>听我的建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her advice about choosing a career proved surprisingly useful.  她关于择业的建议出乎意料地有用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hint</td><td>/ˈhɪnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暗示，提示</td><td>drop a hint<br>透露一点暗示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave me a subtle hint about the surprise party.  她微妙地暗示了那个惊喜派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lead</td><td>/liːd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>引导，带领</td><td>lead a team of interns<br>带领一队实习生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leads new employees through every step of the process.  他带着新员工走完流程的每一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recommendation</td><td>/ˌrekəmənˈdeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>建议，推荐</td><td>make a recommendation<br>提出建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee's recommendation was to postpone the project until spring.  委员会的建议是把项目推迟到春天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tip</td><td>/ˈtɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）倾斜，倾倒</td><td>tip the chair back<br>把椅子向后倾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not tip the glass or the tea will spill.  别把杯子倾斜，茶会洒出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pragmatic</b></td><td>/præɡˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>实用主义的，务实的</td><td>a pragmatic approach<br>务实的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A pragmatic manager focuses on what works rather than on elegant theories.  务实的管理者关注什么有效，而不是漂亮的理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 down-to-earth</td><td>/ˌdaʊn tu ˈɜrθ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>务实的，不摆架子的</td><td>a down-to-earth manner<br>务实的作风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite his fame he remained a down-to-earth neighbor.  尽管成名，他仍是个不摆架子的邻居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 earthy</td><td>/ˈɜːrθi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>实际的，质朴直白的</td><td>earthy common sense<br>质朴的常识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her earthy advice about money helped the young couple enormously.  她关于钱的务实建议对这对年轻夫妇帮助极大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hardheaded</td><td>/ˈhɑːrdˌhedɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；务实精明的</td><td>a hardheaded negotiator<br>强硬的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her hardheaded refusal to compromise stalled the talks for weeks.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使谈判停滞了数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 matter-of-fact</td><td>/ˈmætər əv ˈfækt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>务实的，不动感情的</td><td>a matter-of-fact tone<br>平淡务实的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the accident in a calm, matter-of-fact voice.  他用平静务实的语调描述了事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 practical</td><td>/ˈpræktəkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>实际的，实用的</td><td>a practical solution<br>切实可行的方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need a practical plan, not another beautiful theory.  我们需要切实可行的计划，而不是又一个漂亮理论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fanciful</td><td>/ˈfænsɪfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的，荒谬的</td><td>a fanciful explanation<br>荒谬的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her fanciful account of the accident convinced no one at all.  她对事故荒谬不切实际的描述完全没能说服任何人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 idealistic</td><td>/aɪˌdiːəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理想主义的</td><td>an idealistic student<br>理想主义的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their idealistic plan ignored every practical cost.  他们理想主义的计划忽略了一切实际成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impractical</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpræktəkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的</td><td>an impractical plan<br>不切实际的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building a bridge there proved impractical and hugely expensive.  在那里建桥被证明不切实际且极其昂贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unrealistic</td><td>/ˌʌnriːəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不切实际的</td><td>unrealistic expectations<br>不切实际的期望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their deadline was unrealistic given how little funding remained.  考虑到资金所剩不多，他们的截止期不切实际。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 utopian</td><td>/juˈtoʊpiən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乌托邦式的，空想的</td><td>a utopian scheme<br>空想的方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet proposed a utopian city without money or locks.  这本小册子设想了一座没有钱和锁的空想城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 visionary</td><td>/ˈvɪʒəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；不切实际的，空想的</td><td>a visionary founder<br>有远见的创始人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their visionary founder predicted the mobile market twenty years early.  他们有远见的创始人提前二十年预见了移动市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>premeditate</b></del></td><td>/prɪˈmedɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先考虑</td><td>premeditate the crime<br>预谋犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer argued that his client did not premeditate the attack.  律师主张他的委托人并未预先谋划这场袭击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 forethink</del></td><td>/ˌfɔrˈθɪŋk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先考虑，事先谋划</td><td>forethink the consequences<br>预先考虑后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good commander forethinks every move before the battle begins.  好的指挥官在开战前就预想好每一步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 precogitate</del></td><td>/priˈkɑdʒɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先思考，预谋</td><td>precogitate every step<br>预先思考每一步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thieves had clearly precogitated the escape route in detail.  窃贼显然事先详细谋划了逃跑路线。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predetermine</td><td>/ˌpriːdɪˈtɜːrmən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先决定，预先确定</td><td>predetermine the result<br>预先决定结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Genes do not entirely predetermine a person's health.  基因并不完全预先决定一个人的健康。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disregard</td><td>/ˌdɪsrɪˈɡɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>漠视，不关注；缺乏兴趣，缺乏关心</td><td>total disregard for safety<br>完全不顾安全</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their total disregard for safety rules eventually caused a serious accident.  他们完全不顾安全规定，最终造成了一起严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignore</td><td>/ˌɪɡˈnɔːr/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视，不理</td><td>ignore the warning<br>忽视警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain ignored the warning and sailed into the storm.  船长忽视了警告，驶入了风暴之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 overlook</td><td>/ˈoʊvərˌlʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>忽视；宽恕；俯视</td><td>overlook a minor error<br>宽容小错</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher chose to overlook the boy's late arrival.  老师决定不追究那男孩的迟到。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prerequisite</b></td><td>/ˌpriːˈrekwəzɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>先决条件，前提</td><td>a prerequisite for admission<br>入学的先决条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Basic statistics is a prerequisite for admission to the graduate program.  基础统计学是进入该研究生项目的先决条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condition</td><td>/kənˈdɪʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使适应，训练；条件</td><td>be conditioned to expect<br>被训练成习惯期待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Athletes are conditioned to endure long periods of pain.  运动员被训练得能长时间承受痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 essential</td><td>/ɪˈsenʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本质的，必不可少的</td><td>an essential quality<br>本质的特性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is an essential part of her character.  耐心是她性格中本质的一部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 must</td><td>/ˈmʌst/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>必须做的事，必备之物</td><td>an absolute must<br>绝对必备之物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm coat is a must if you visit Chicago.  去芝加哥的话厚外套是必备的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 necessity</td><td>/nəˈsesəti/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>必需品；必要，需要</td><td>a basic necessity<br>一件基本必需品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clean water is a necessity, not a luxury.  清洁饮水是必需品，不是奢侈品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precondition</td><td>/ˌpriːkənˈdɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>前提条件，先决条件</td><td>a precondition for talks<br>会谈的前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A ceasefire was the precondition for any serious negotiation.  停火是任何认真谈判的前提。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requirement</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪrmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>要求，必要条件</td><td>meet the requirements<br>满足这些要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two years of experience is a requirement for this post.  两年经验是这个职位的必要条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requisite</td><td>/ˈrekwɪzɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>必需品；必要条件</td><td>requisite skills for the job<br>工作所需的技能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is a requisite for anyone hoping to teach very young children.  耐心是任何想教幼儿的人必备的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sine qua non</td><td>/ˌsɪni kwɑ ˈnɑn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>必要条件，必不可少之物</td><td>a sine qua non<br>必不可少的条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is the sine qua non of any lasting partnership.  信任是任何长久合作必不可少的条件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prerogative</b></td><td>/prɪˈrɑːɡətɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>特权，权力</td><td>the royal prerogative<br>王室特权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Granting pardons remains the prerogative of the president alone.  赦免权仍然是总统独有的特权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 appanage</del></td><td>/ˈæpənɪdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>应得的权利；封地收入</td><td>the appanage of rank<br>身份带来的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A private carriage was once the appanage of every duke.  私人马车曾是每位公爵应得的特权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 birthright</td><td>/ˈbɜːrθˌraɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>与生俱来的权利</td><td>a citizen's birthright<br>公民的天赋权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free speech is the birthright of every person in this country.  言论自由是这个国家每个人生来的权利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entitlement</td><td>/enˈtaɪtəlmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>应得的权利，资格</td><td>a sense of entitlement<br>理所应得的心态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Veterans lost several entitlements when the budget was cut.  预算削减时退伍军人失去了几项应得权益。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 privilege</td><td>/ˈprɪvlədʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>特权，优待</td><td>a special privilege<br>特殊的优待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Members enjoy the privilege of borrowing rare books.  会员享有借阅珍本书的特权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 right</td><td>/ˈraɪt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词、名词</td><td>正确的；正常的，妥当的</td><td>put things right<br>把事情弄妥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You were right about the weather turning cold tonight.  你说今晚天气会变冷，说对了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proclaim</b></td><td>/prəˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣告，使…公之于众</td><td>proclaim independence<br>宣布独立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The colony voted to proclaim independence from the empire that summer.  那年夏天，殖民地投票宣告脱离帝国独立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advertise</td><td>/ˈædvərˌtaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>宣传，为…做广告</td><td>advertise a new brand<br>宣传新品牌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They advertised the concert on every bus in the city.  他们在全城每辆公交上宣传这场音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 announce</td><td>/əˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣布，公布</td><td>announce the results<br>公布结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor will announce her decision at tomorrow's press conference.  市长将在明天的记者会上宣布决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blare</td><td>/ˈbler/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>响亮刺耳的声音；轰响</td><td>the blare of trumpets<br>响亮刺耳的号声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Music blared from the open windows of the passing car.  音乐从驶过汽车敞开的车窗里轰响出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blazon</td><td>/ˈbleɪzn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使知名；修饰，装扮</td><td>blazon the news abroad<br>四处宣扬消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper was eager to blazon the news across every front page.  该报急于把这条消息在各家头版上四处宣扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 broadcast</td><td>/ˈbrɔːdˌkæst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>广播，播送；散布</td><td>broadcast the news<br>播报消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The station broadcast the news of the rescue every hour.  电台每小时播报一次营救消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 declare</td><td>/dɪˈkler/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>宣布，声明，断言</td><td>declare one's intentions<br>表明自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge declared the earlier verdict invalid and ordered a retrial.  法官宣布先前判决无效并下令重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enunciate</td><td>/ɪˈnʌnsieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、及物动词</td><td>清晰地说；公开宣布，宣称</td><td>enunciate each word clearly<br>清晰吐出每个词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please enunciate each word clearly so the people at the back can hear.  请把每个词清晰地吐出，好让后排的人听清。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 herald</td><td>/ˈherəld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>预示，预兆</td><td>herald a new era<br>预示新时代来临</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invention heralded a new era in communication.  这项发明预示着通信新时代的到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promulgate</td><td>/ˈprɑːməlɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式宣布</td><td>promulgate a new law<br>颁布新法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government will promulgate a new law on data privacy next month.  政府将于下月正式颁布一项新的数据隐私法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 publicize</td><td>/ˈpʌblɪsaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣传，公布</td><td>publicize the event<br>宣传这场活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Organizers publicized the concert with posters across the whole campus.  组织者用海报在全校宣传这场音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 publish</td><td>/ˈpʌblɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公布，发表；出版</td><td>publish the findings<br>公布研究结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team plans to publish its results in a major journal.  团队计划在一份重要期刊上发表结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 release</td><td>/riˈliːs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>释放，解除束缚；发布</td><td>release the prisoners<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ordered officials to release him from custody at once.  法庭命令官员立即将他从拘留中释放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trumpet</td><td>/ˈtrʌmpɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>大声说出或宣告</td><td>trumpet their success<br>大肆宣扬他们的成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company trumpeted its record profits in every newspaper that week.  那一周公司在各家报纸上大肆宣扬其创纪录的利润。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conceal</td><td>/kənˈsiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>conceal the truth<br>隐瞒真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness tried to conceal the truth from the police.  证人试图向警方隐瞒真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hide</td><td>/ˈhaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>隐藏，隐瞒</td><td>hide the evidence<br>藏匿证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hid the letters in a drawer beneath her sweaters.  她把信藏在毛衣下面的抽屉里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>project</b></td><td>/prəˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突出，伸出</td><td>project from the wall<br>从墙面伸出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stone ledge projects from the wall about two meters up.  一道石台从墙面约两米高处伸出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 augur</td><td>/ˈɔːɡər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>预言家，占卜官；预言</td><td>augur well for the future<br>预示前景良好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The Roman augur studied the flight of birds before every battle.  这位罗马占卜官在每场战役前都观察鸟的飞行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 estimate</td><td>/ˈestəmət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>估计，估算</td><td>a rough estimate<br>粗略的估算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave only a rough estimate of the repair costs.  她只给出了修理费用的粗略估算。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extrapolate</td><td>/ɪkˈstræpəleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过逻辑）推断</td><td>extrapolate from limited data<br>由有限数据推断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Analysts extrapolate future growth from limited historical economic data.  分析师根据有限的历史经济数据推断未来增长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forecast</td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌkæst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>预测，预报</td><td>forecast heavy rain<br>预报大雨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Economists forecast slower growth for the coming two years.  经济学家预测未来两年增长会放缓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predict</td><td>/prɪˈdɪkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预言，预测</td><td>predict the outcome<br>预测结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could predict how the jury would finally vote.  没人能预测陪审团最终会如何表决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predetermine</td><td>/ˌpriːdɪˈtɜːrmən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先决定，预先确定</td><td>predetermine the result<br>预先决定结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Genes do not entirely predetermine a person's health.  基因并不完全预先决定一个人的健康。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presage</td><td>/ˈpresɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>征兆</td><td>presage of disaster<br>灾难的征兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden silence of the birds was a presage of the storm.  鸟鸣骤然停止，是暴风雨来临的征兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proprietary</b></td><td>/prəˈpraɪəteri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>专有的，专利的</td><td>proprietary software<br>专有软件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company refuses to share its proprietary software with outside developers.  这家公司拒绝向外部开发者提供其私有软件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 private</td><td>/ˈpraɪvət/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>私下的，秘密的</td><td>a private meeting<br>私下的会面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They held a private meeting before the public hearing.  他们在公开听证会前举行了私下会面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 public</td><td>/ˈpʌblɪk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>公开的；公众的</td><td>make it public<br>予以公开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company made its findings public earlier this month.  公司本月早些时候公开了调查结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prototype</b></td><td>/ˈproʊtətaɪp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原型</td><td>a working prototype<br>可运行的原型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers tested a working prototype of the electric bus last spring.  工程师去年春天测试了这款电动公交车的可用原型。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archetype</td><td>/ˈɑːrkɪtaɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原型，典型</td><td>the archetype of a hero<br>英雄的典型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sherlock Holmes remains the archetype of the brilliant private detective.  夏洛克·福尔摩斯仍是天才私家侦探的原型。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 copy</td><td>/ˈkɑːpi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品，仿制品；抄写</td><td>a cheap copy<br>廉价仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting on the wall is only a cheap copy.  墙上那幅画只是廉价的仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imitation</td><td>/ˌɪmɪˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>仿制品</td><td>a cheap imitation<br>廉价的仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bag was a cheap imitation sold openly at the night market.  那只包是夜市上公开出售的廉价仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 replica</td><td>/ˈreplɪkə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复制品，仿制品</td><td>a full-size replica<br>原尺寸的复制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Visitors climb a wooden replica of the ancient tower.  游客可以爬上那座古塔的木制复制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reproduction</td><td>/ˌriːprəˈdʌkʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复制品；复制</td><td>a cheap reproduction<br>廉价的复制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He hung a reproduction of the famous painting above his desk.  他在书桌上方挂了那幅名画的复制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>rampant</b></td><td>/ˈræmpənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（植物）生长茂盛的；猖獗的，不受限制的</td><td>rampant inflation<br>猖獗的通货膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rampant inflation wiped out the savings of ordinary families that year.  那一年猖獗的通货膨胀吞掉了普通家庭的储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lush</td><td>/lʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>lush vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lush vegetation covered the valley floor after weeks of steady rain.  连续数周的降雨之后，茂盛的植被覆盖了谷底。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 luxuriant</td><td>/lʌɡˈʒʊriənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>luxuriant vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Luxuriant ferns covered the damp floor of the forest.  茂盛的蕨类覆盖了潮湿的林地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prosperous</td><td>/ˈprɑːspərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>成功的，繁盛的</td><td>a prosperous merchant town<br>繁盛的商镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harbor grew into a prosperous merchant town within a single generation.  这个港口在一代人的时间里发展成繁盛的商镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weedy</td><td>/ˈwiːdi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>杂草丛生的，蔓生的</td><td>a weedy garden<br>杂草丛生的花园</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The weedy lot behind the school had not been mowed all summer.  学校后面那块杂草丛生的空地整夏未修剪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abandoned</td><td>/əˈbændənd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>放纵的，肆意的；被弃的</td><td>abandoned behavior<br>放纵的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancers moved with an abandoned energy that thrilled the crowd.  舞者以放纵的活力舞动，令观众兴奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intemperate</td><td>/ɪnˈtempərət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无节制的，极端的，不温和的</td><td>intemperate language<br>过激的言辞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His intemperate language during the debate cost him the support of moderates.  他在辩论中过激的言辞使他失去了温和派的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 runaway</td><td>/ˈrʌnəˌweɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>失控的，飞涨的</td><td>runaway inflation<br>失控的通货膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Runaway costs forced the city to cancel the new stadium.  失控的成本迫使该市取消新体育场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbounded</td><td>/ʌnˈbaʊndɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无限的，无节制的</td><td>unbounded enthusiasm<br>无限的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children greeted the snow with unbounded delight.  孩子们以无限的喜悦迎接这场雪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unbridled</td><td>/ʌnˈbraɪdəld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>放纵的，不受约束的</td><td>unbridled ambition<br>不受约束的野心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unbridled spending soon emptied the family savings.  他毫无节制的花钱很快耗尽了家里的储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unchecked</td><td>/ʌnˈtʃekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受制止的，任其发展的</td><td>grow unchecked<br>无阻碍地生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Left unchecked, the vines will cover the entire wall.  若不加制止，藤蔓会覆盖整面墙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncontrolled</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈtroʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受控制的</td><td>uncontrolled growth<br>不受控制的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Uncontrolled logging has destroyed much of the coastal forest.  不受控制的伐木毁掉了大片沿海森林。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unhampered</td><td>/ʌnˈhæmpərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受阻碍的</td><td>unhampered by rules<br>不受规则束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unhampered by traffic, the ambulance reached the hospital in minutes.  救护车未受交通阻碍，几分钟就到了医院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unhindered</td><td>/ʌnˈhɪndərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未受妨碍的，畅通的</td><td>unhindered access<br>不受阻的通行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters demanded unhindered access to the disaster site.  记者要求不受阻碍地进入灾区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unrestrained</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不受约束的；无节制的</td><td>unrestrained spending<br>无节制的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of unrestrained spending finally ruined the company.  多年无节制的开支最终毁掉了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sparse</td><td>/spɑːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀疏的，稀少的</td><td>sparse vegetation<br>稀疏的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vegetation grows sparse as the trail climbs above the tree line.  小径爬上林线以上后，植被变得稀疏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bridled</td><td>/ˈbraɪdəld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受约束的，被抑制的</td><td>bridled anger<br>受抑制的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His bridled temper showed only in the tightness of his voice.  他受抑制的怒气只表现在声音的紧绷上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 checked</td><td>/ˈtʃekt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受制止的，被控制的</td><td>checked growth<br>受抑制的增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 With spending checked, the department finally balanced its budget.  开支得到控制后，该部门终于收支平衡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constrained</td><td>/kənˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>拘谨的，不自然的；受限的</td><td>a constrained smile<br>一个勉强的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their conversation was polite but oddly constrained.  他们的谈话礼貌，却出奇地拘谨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 controlled</td><td>/kənˈtroʊld/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受控制的，受管制的</td><td>a controlled experiment<br>受控实验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prices stayed low under a tightly controlled market.  在严格管控的市场中价格保持低位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 curbed</td><td>/ˈkɜːrbd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>被抑制的，受约束的</td><td>curbed enthusiasm<br>受到抑制的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curbed by strict rules, the students rarely spoke in class.  受严格规定约束，学生在课上很少发言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 governed</td><td>/ˈɡʌvərnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受管辖的，被支配的</td><td>governed by law<br>受法律管辖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every transaction here is governed by federal banking rules.  这里每笔交易都受联邦银行规则管辖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hampered</td><td>/ˈhæmpərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受阻碍的，被妨碍的</td><td>hampered by fog<br>受大雾阻碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The search was hampered by darkness and heavy rain.  搜寻受到黑暗和大雨的阻碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hindered</td><td>/ˈhɪndərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>受妨碍的，被阻挡的</td><td>hindered progress<br>受阻的进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repairs were hindered by a shortage of skilled workers.  维修因缺乏熟练工人而受阻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 restrained</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>受约束的，克制的</td><td>restrained by rules<br>受规则约束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His restrained reply hid how angry he actually felt inside.  他克制的回答掩盖了他内心真正的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ravenous</b></td><td>/ˈrævənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>食量大的，贪食的；贪婪的</td><td>a ravenous appetite<br>极大的食量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long hike the boys had a ravenous appetite for anything.  长途徒步之后，男孩们对任何食物都胃口极大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 edacious</del></td><td>/ɪˈdeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的，暴食的</td><td>an edacious appetite<br>贪吃的胃口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The edacious puppy emptied both bowls in a minute.  贪吃的小狗一分钟就吃空了两个碗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 esurient</del></td><td>/ɪˈsʊriənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>饥饿的，贪食的</td><td>esurient guests<br>饥肠辘辘的客人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Esurient travelers cleared the table before the host sat.  饥饿的旅客在主人坐下前就吃光了满桌菜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gluttonous</td><td>/ˈɡlʌtənəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的，暴食的</td><td>gluttonous habits<br>暴食的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The emperor's gluttonous feasts lasted for several days.  皇帝暴食的宴席持续了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 greedy</td><td>/ˈɡriːdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪婪的，贪心的</td><td>greedy for money<br>贪财</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew greedy and lost everything in one bad deal.  他变得贪心，在一笔糟糕的交易中失去了一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rapacious</td><td>/rəˈpeɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度贪婪的</td><td>rapacious business practices<br>贪婪的商业行径</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rapacious business practices drove several small competitors out of the market.  贪婪的商业行径把几家小竞争者挤出了市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 voracious</td><td>/vəˈreɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的；如饥似渴的</td><td>a voracious appetite<br>极大的食欲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a voracious reader who finishes a novel each week.  他是个如饥似渴的读者，每周读完一本小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 content</td><td>/ˈkɑːntent/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>内容，内涵；使满足</td><td>the content of the letter<br>信的内容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher praised the style but questioned the content of my essay.  老师赞赏我文章的文风，却质疑其内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sated</td><td>/ˈseɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>满足的，饱足的</td><td>sated with food<br>吃得饱足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sated after the long dinner, nobody wanted dessert.  长长的晚餐后大家都饱了，没人要甜点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 satiated</td><td>/ˈseɪʃieɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>饱足的，厌腻的</td><td>satiated with luxury<br>对奢侈已厌腻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers satiated with sequels ignored the studio's newest film.  对续集已厌腻的观众忽略了片方的新片。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 satisfied</td><td>/ˈsætəsˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>满意的，满足的</td><td>a satisfied customer<br>满意的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She looked satisfied with the results of the experiment.  她对实验结果看上去很满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>redeem</b></td><td>/rɪˈdiːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改过自新</td><td>redeem oneself through service<br>以服务改过自新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He hoped to redeem himself through years of quiet public service.  他希望通过多年默默的公共服务来改过自新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 habilitate</del></td><td>/həˈbɪlɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使具备能力，使胜任</td><td>habilitate disabled workers<br>使残障工人具备工作能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The center exists to habilitate young adults with learning difficulties.  该中心旨在使有学习障碍的青年具备生活能力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reclaim</td><td>/riˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感化，使改造；开垦，回收</td><td>reclaim young offenders<br>感化少年犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity tries to reclaim addicts through work and counseling.  该慈善机构试图通过工作和咨询感化成瘾者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regenerate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重获新生，使焕然一新</td><td>regenerate a neighborhood<br>使一个街区焕然一新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city spent millions to regenerate a neighborhood near the old port.  该市花费数百万使旧港附近的一个街区焕然一新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rehabilitate</td><td>/ˌriːhəˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复原，使康复</td><td>rehabilitate former inmates<br>帮助刑满释放者重返社会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The program aims to rehabilitate former inmates and find them jobs.  该项目旨在帮刑满释放者重返社会并找到工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>refreshing</b></td><td>/rɪˈfreʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人身心振奋的，提神的</td><td>a refreshing drink<br>提神的饮料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cold shower is wonderfully refreshing after a long run.  长跑后洗个冷水澡格外提神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bracing</td><td>/ˈbreɪsɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人振奋的，提神的</td><td>a bracing sea breeze<br>令人振奋的海风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bracing wind off the lake woke us up immediately.  湖面吹来的清冽寒风立刻让我们清醒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cordial</td><td>/ˈkɔːrdʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情友好的；令人振奋的</td><td>a cordial welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two leaders exchanged cordial greetings before the talks.  两位领导人在会谈前互致亲切的问候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invigorating</td><td>/ɪnˈvɪɡəreɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人精神焕发的</td><td>an invigorating morning walk<br>令人振奋的晨间散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cold shower can be surprisingly invigorating after a run.  跑步后洗个冷水澡出奇地让人精神焕发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenating</td><td>/rɪˈdʒuːvəneɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使恢复活力的</td><td>a rejuvenating holiday<br>让人恢复活力的假期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two weeks by the lake proved wonderfully rejuvenating.  在湖边的两周让人格外恢复了活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restorative</td><td>/rɪˈstɔːrətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a restorative sleep<br>恢复元气的睡眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long walk by the sea proved wonderfully restorative after weeks of stress.  数周压力之后，在海边长时间散步格外有益身心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reviving</td><td>/rɪˈvaɪvɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>使苏醒的，令人重振的</td><td>a reviving cup of tea<br>一杯提神的茶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She handed him a reviving cup of strong coffee.  她给他递上一杯提神的浓咖啡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulating</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>激励的，令人振奋的</td><td>a stimulating discussion<br>一场激发思考的讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The seminar was the most stimulating class of the term.  这场研讨会是本学期最能激发思考的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulative</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪtɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有激励作用的，令人振奋的</td><td>a stimulative effect<br>激励作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cold morning air had a stimulative effect on the runners.  清冷的晨风对跑者有振奋作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitalizing</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使有活力的，提神的</td><td>a vitalizing sea breeze<br>令人振奋的海风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mountain air had a vitalizing effect on the patients.  山间空气对病人有振奋精神的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deadening</td><td>/ˈdedənɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人麻木的，使人乏味的</td><td>a deadening routine<br>令人麻木的日常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of deadening office work had dulled his curiosity.  多年令人麻木的办公室工作磨钝了他的好奇心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 debilitating</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>使人虚弱的</td><td>a debilitating illness<br>使人虚弱的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A debilitating fever kept him in bed for weeks.  一场使人虚弱的高烧让他卧床数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 draining</td><td>/ˈdreɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗尽精力的</td><td>emotionally draining work<br>耗费心力的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for a sick parent is emotionally draining work.  照顾生病的父母是耗费心力的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enervating</td><td>/ˈenərveɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使人虚弱无力的</td><td>enervating summer heat<br>令人虚脱的暑热</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The enervating heat made everyone slow and irritable.  令人虚脱的暑热使人人迟缓易怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enfeebling</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiblɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使衰弱的</td><td>an enfeebling disease<br>使人衰弱的疾病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of poor diet had an enfeebling effect on him.  多年饮食不良使他身体日渐衰弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhausting</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>使人极度疲劳的</td><td>an exhausting journey<br>累人的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The twelve-hour shift was exhausting for the young nurses.  十二小时的班让年轻护士精疲力竭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sapping</td><td>/ˈsæpɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>耗损精力的</td><td>energy-sapping humidity<br>耗人精力的湿热</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The energy-sapping humidity left the workers exhausted by noon.  耗人精力的湿热让工人们到中午就疲惫不堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wearying</td><td>/ˈwɪriɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人疲倦的</td><td>a wearying journey<br>令人疲倦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wearying meeting dragged on for almost four hours.  这场令人疲倦的会议持续了近四小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>remnant</b></td><td>/ˈremnənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>残余，剩余物</td><td>the last remnants of<br>…最后的残余</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a few remnants of the ancient wall still stand today.  今天只剩下古城墙的少量残余仍然矗立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debris</td><td>/dəˈbriː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>碎片，残骸</td><td>clear away the debris<br>清理废墟碎片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rescuers dug through the debris for hours looking for survivors.  救援人员在废墟中挖掘了数小时，寻找生还者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remainder</td><td>/rɪˈmeɪndər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>剩余部分，余数</td><td>the remainder of the day<br>这一天余下的时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent the remainder of the money on books.  我们把剩下的钱都花在买书上了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remains</td><td>/rɪˈmeɪnz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>剩余物，残迹；遗体</td><td>the remains of dinner<br>晚餐的剩余</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Archaeologists found the remains of a Roman bathhouse here.  考古学家在此发现了一座罗马浴场的遗迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 residue</td><td>/ˈrezɪduː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>剩余物</td><td>a sticky residue<br>黏稠的残留物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dark residue remained at the bottom of the flask.  烧瓶底部留下了深色的残留物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vestige</td><td>/ˈvestɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>微小的遗迹，小痕迹</td><td>the last vestige of hope<br>最后一丝希望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a vestige of the ancient wall remains beside the modern road.  现代公路旁只剩下古城墙的一点遗迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>renege</b></td><td>/rɪˈniːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>背弃（承诺），反悔</td><td>renege on a promise<br>背弃承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reneged on its promise to raise wages.  公司背弃了加薪的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forswear</td><td>/fɔrˈswer/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃，弃绝</td><td>forswear alcohol<br>发誓戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forswore gambling after losing all his savings.  输掉全部积蓄后他发誓戒赌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recant</td><td>/rɪˈkænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤回，公开放弃（说法）</td><td>recant a confession<br>撤回供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure he agreed to recant his earlier statement.  在压力下他同意撤回先前的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repeal</td><td>/rɪˈpiːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除（法律等）</td><td>repeal the old law<br>废除旧法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers voted to repeal the outdated statute that had confused courts for decades.  议员们投票废除了这部困扰法院数十年的过时法规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retract</td><td>/rɪˈtrækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>收回，否认</td><td>retract a statement<br>撤回一项声明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The newspaper agreed to retract the claim and print a formal apology.  报社同意收回那项说法并刊登正式道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdraw</td><td>/wɪðˈdrɔː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取出（款项）；撤回，撤退</td><td>withdraw money from the bank<br>从银行取钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stopped at the bank to withdraw money before the long trip.  他在长途旅行前去银行取钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adhere</td><td>/ədˈhɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>adhere to the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All staff must adhere to the safety procedures without exception.  全体员工必须无例外地遵守安全规程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>replenish</b></td><td>/rɪˈplenɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充</td><td>replenish supplies<br>补充物资</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must replenish the water tanks before crossing the desert.  穿越沙漠前我们必须把水箱补满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refill</td><td>/riˈfɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>再填满，补充</td><td>refill the glass<br>把杯子再倒满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter refilled our cups without being asked.  服务员没等我们要求就把杯子添满了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reload</td><td>/riˈloʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新装载，再装填</td><td>reload the truck<br>重新装车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hunter reloaded his rifle before moving up the ridge.  猎人在上山脊前重新装填了步枪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 consume</td><td>/kənˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>消耗，耗尽</td><td>consume vast amounts of energy<br>消耗大量能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Data centers consume vast amounts of electricity every single day.  数据中心每天消耗大量电力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 drain</td><td>/ˈdreɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>耗尽，使枯竭</td><td>drain the country's resources<br>耗尽国家资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long war drained the country's resources and its morale.  漫长的战争耗尽了国家的资源和士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 empty</td><td>/ˈempti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>空洞的；空的</td><td>empty promises<br>空洞的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech was full of empty phrases about progress.  他的演讲满是关于进步的空洞辞藻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exhaust</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>耗尽</td><td>exhaust the water supply<br>耗尽水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two dry summers exhausted the town's entire water supply.  连续两个干燥的夏天耗尽了全镇的水源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>replicate</b></td><td>/ˈreplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>复制；重复（实验）</td><td>replicate the experiment exactly<br>精确重复该实验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two other laboratories failed to replicate the results of the original experiment.  另外两个实验室未能重复出原实验的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clone</td><td>/ˈkloʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>复制品，翻版</td><td>a cheap clone<br>廉价的翻版</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new phone is basically a clone of last year's model.  这款新手机基本上是去年型号的翻版。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copy</td><td>/ˈkɑːpi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品，仿制品；抄写</td><td>a cheap copy<br>廉价仿制品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting on the wall is only a cheap copy.  墙上那幅画只是廉价的仿制品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 copycat</td><td>/ˈkɑːpiˌkæt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>盲目模仿者，抄袭者</td><td>a copycat crime<br>模仿性的犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine was a shameless copycat of a French title.  这本杂志无耻地照抄了一份法国刊物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 duplicate</td><td>/ˈduːpləkət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>复制品</td><td>an exact duplicate<br>完全一样的副本</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep an exact duplicate of the contract in a separate locked drawer.  把合同的一份完全一样的副本另放在一个上锁的抽屉里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imitate</td><td>/ˈɪməˌteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>模仿，仿效</td><td>imitate his accent<br>模仿他的口音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children naturally imitate the speech of adults around them.  孩子会自然地模仿身边大人的说话方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 reduplicate</del></td><td>/riˈduːplɪkeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重复，加倍复制</td><td>reduplicate the same error<br>重复同样的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Later editions merely reduplicated the printer's earlier mistakes.  后来的版本只是重复了印工先前的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 render</td><td>/ˈrendər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>交出，给予；使成为</td><td>render assistance<br>提供援助</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighbors rendered assistance long before the ambulance arrived.  邻居们在救护车到达前很久就提供了援助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproduce</td><td>/ˌriːprəˈduːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>复制，再现；繁殖</td><td>reproduce the painting faithfully<br>忠实复制这幅画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The catalog reproduces every drawing in full color.  图录以全彩复制了每一幅素描。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 originate</td><td>/əˈrɪdʒəneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>创始，首创；起源</td><td>originate a new style<br>首创一种新风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She originated a teaching method now used across the country.  她首创了一种如今全国通用的教学法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>residual</b></td><td>/rɪˈzɪdʒuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>剩余的，残存的</td><td>residual traces of paint<br>残存的颜料痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Residual damp in the wall ruined the new plaster.  墙里残存的湿气毁了新抹的灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leftover</td><td>/ˈlefˌtoʊvər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>剩余的，残留的</td><td>leftover food<br>剩下的食物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We ate leftover pizza for breakfast the next morning.  第二天早上我们吃剩下的比萨当早餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remaining</td><td>/rɪˈmeɪnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>剩下的，剩余的</td><td>the remaining time<br>剩下的时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spend the remaining money on books rather than snacks.  把剩下的钱花在书上而不是零食上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vestigial</td><td>/vəˈstɪdʒiəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>残留的，退化的</td><td>a vestigial organ<br>退化的器官</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whales retain vestigial hip bones from their walking ancestors.  鲸保留着来自行走祖先的退化髋骨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 comprehensive</td><td>/ˌkɑːmprɪˈhensɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>全面的，综合的</td><td>a comprehensive survey<br>全面的调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee produced a comprehensive report on water quality.  委员会就水质提出了一份全面的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 entire</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>全部的，完整的</td><td>the entire collection<br>整批收藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She read the entire manuscript in a single night.  她一夜之间读完了整部手稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 full</td><td>/ˈfʊl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>完整的，充满的</td><td>a full account<br>完整的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a full explanation of the accident to police.  他向警方对事故作了完整说明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 integral</td><td>/ˈɪntɪɡrəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可缺少的；完整的</td><td>an integral part<br>不可缺少的部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careful revision is an integral part of writing any serious research paper.  仔细修改是撰写任何严肃研究论文不可缺少的部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 total</td><td>/ˈtoʊtəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>全部的，总计的</td><td>a total failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The renovation cost a total of two million dollars.  翻修总共花费两百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 whole</td><td>/ˈhoʊl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>整个的，完整的</td><td>the whole truth<br>全部真相</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the whole afternoon organizing his notes.  他花了整个下午整理笔记。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>restitution</b></td><td>/ˌrestɪˈtuːʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>补偿，赔偿</td><td>make restitution to victims<br>向受害者作出赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ordered the company to make full restitution to the injured workers.  法院命令公司向受伤工人做出全额赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 damages</td><td>/ˈdæmədʒəz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>损害赔偿金</td><td>sue for damages<br>起诉索赔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court awarded her substantial damages for the injury.  法院判给她一笔可观的伤害赔偿金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indemnity</td><td>/ɪnˈdemnəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（损害、伤害等的）保险补偿</td><td>claim an indemnity<br>索取保险赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The insurance company paid a generous indemnity for the damage caused by the flood.  保险公司为洪水造成的损失支付了一笔丰厚的赔偿金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 quittance</del></td><td>/ˈkwɪtəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>债务免除；补偿，报答</td><td>a quittance for the debt<br>债务的免除凭据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The merchant signed a quittance for the remaining sum.  商人为余款签署了一份免除凭据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recompense</td><td>/ˈrekəmˌpens/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>补偿，报酬</td><td>recompense for the loss<br>对损失的补偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was given a small sum as recompense for her trouble.  她得到一小笔钱作为麻烦的补偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recoupment</td><td>/rɪˈkupmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>补偿，弥补损失</td><td>recoupment of losses<br>损失的补偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawsuit sought recoupment of every dollar the firm lost.  诉讼要求补偿公司损失的每一美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redress</td><td>/rɪˈdres/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>赔偿，纠正</td><td>seek legal redress<br>寻求法律赔偿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Victims went to court to seek redress for their losses.  受害者诉诸法庭为损失寻求赔偿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remuneration</td><td>/rɪˌmjuːnəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报酬，酬金</td><td>generous remuneration<br>丰厚的报酬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He accepted the post despite the modest remuneration.  尽管报酬不高，他还是接受了这个职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reparation</td><td>/ˌrepəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赔偿，补偿</td><td>pay war reparations<br>支付战争赔款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated state was forced to pay heavy reparations.  战败国被迫支付巨额赔款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprisal</td><td>/riˈpraɪzəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报复，报复行动</td><td>fear of reprisal<br>害怕遭到报复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses stayed silent for fear of reprisal.  证人们因害怕报复而保持沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 requital</td><td>/rɪˈkwaɪtəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>报答，补偿；报复</td><td>requital for his kindness<br>对他好意的报答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sent a gift in requital for their long hospitality.  她送去礼物以报答他们长久的款待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>resurrect</b></td><td>/ˌrezəˈrekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重生，使复苏</td><td>resurrect an old idea<br>重提一个旧想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director hopes to resurrect a play that failed thirty years ago.  新导演希望让三十年前失败的一部戏重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reanimate</td><td>/riˈænɪˌmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>reanimate the debate<br>使争论重新活跃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single letter reanimated a friendship dormant for years.  一封信让沉寂多年的友谊重新活了过来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recharge</td><td>/riˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>充电；恢复精力</td><td>recharge his batteries<br>恢复精力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A quiet weekend in the mountains recharged him completely.  在山里过了个安静的周末，他完全恢复了精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regenerate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒenəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使重获新生，使焕然一新</td><td>regenerate a neighborhood<br>使一个街区焕然一新</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city spent millions to regenerate a neighborhood near the old port.  该市花费数百万使旧港附近的一个街区焕然一新。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejuvenate</td><td>/rɪˈdʒuːvəˌneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使显得年轻，使恢复活力</td><td>rejuvenate the old district<br>使老城区恢复活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two weeks by the sea seemed to rejuvenate my grandmother.  在海边待了两周，我外婆好像年轻了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rekindle</td><td>/riˈkɪndəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新点燃，重新激起</td><td>rekindle interest<br>重新激起兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary rekindled public interest in deep-sea exploration.  这部纪录片重新激起了公众对深海探索的兴趣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renew</td><td>/rɪˈnuː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>补充更新，续期</td><td>renew the water supply<br>补充水源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spring rains renewed the reservoir after a long dry summer.  春雨在漫长干旱的夏季后补满了水库。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resuscitate</td><td>/rɪˈsʌsɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使苏醒</td><td>resuscitate a drowning victim<br>抢救一名溺水者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Paramedics worked for twenty minutes to resuscitate the man pulled from the lake.  急救人员花了二十分钟抢救从湖里救上来的男子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revitalize</td><td>/riˈvaɪtəˌlaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恢复生机，使复兴</td><td>revitalize the local economy<br>振兴当地经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new library helped revitalize a neighborhood everyone had abandoned.  新图书馆帮助振兴了一个人人都已放弃的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 revive</td><td>/rɪˈvaɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>再获新生</td><td>revive after the crisis<br>危机后重获生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Local trade began to revive once the new highway opened last spring.  去年春天新高速开通后，当地贸易开始重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 revivify</del></td><td>/riˈvɪvɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复活，使重新振作</td><td>revivify a dying tradition<br>使一项衰落的传统复兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh funding revivified a research program left dormant for years.  新的资金使一个沉寂多年的研究项目重获生机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 kill</td><td>/ˈkɪl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否决，取消（议案）</td><td>kill the bill<br>否决议案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senate killed the bill on its second reading.  参议院在二读时否决了这项议案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retentive</b></td><td>/rɪˈtentɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>记性好的</td><td>a retentive memory<br>过人的记忆力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A retentive memory helped her recite the entire poem after one reading.  过人的记忆力使她读一遍就能背出整首诗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absentminded</td><td>/ˌæbsəntˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心不在焉的，健忘的</td><td>an absentminded professor<br>心不在焉的教授</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My absentminded uncle left his keys in the front door.  我心不在焉的叔叔把钥匙忘在前门上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forgetful</td><td>/fɔrˈɡetfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>健忘的，记性差的</td><td>grow forgetful<br>变得健忘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grandmother has become forgetful about names but still remembers songs.  奶奶记不住名字了，却仍记得歌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 oblivious</td><td>/əˈblɪviəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，不知情的</td><td>oblivious to the noise<br>察觉不到噪音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Deep in her book, she was oblivious to the noise of the crowd.  她沉浸在书里，察觉不到人群的嘈杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>retrieve</b></td><td>/rɪˈtriːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>寻回，找回</td><td>retrieve lost data<br>找回丢失的数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineer managed to retrieve the files from the damaged hard drive.  工程师成功从损坏的硬盘中找回了文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reacquire</td><td>/riəˈkwaɪr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新获得，再取得</td><td>reacquire the rights<br>重新取得权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum hoped to reacquire the painting sold in 1930.  博物馆希望重新买回1930年售出的那幅画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recapture</td><td>/riˈkæptʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>夺回，重新捕获</td><td>recapture the city<br>夺回城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team recaptured the lead in the final ten minutes.  球队在最后十分钟重新夺回领先。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reclaim</td><td>/riˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>感化，使改造；开垦，回收</td><td>reclaim young offenders<br>感化少年犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity tries to reclaim addicts through work and counseling.  该慈善机构试图通过工作和咨询感化成瘾者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 re-collect</td><td>/ˌri kəˈlɛkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新收集，重新收拢</td><td>re-collect one's thoughts<br>重新理清思绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She paused to re-collect her thoughts before answering the question.  她停下重新理清思绪，然后回答问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recoup</td><td>/rɪˈkuːp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>恢复，复原；补偿</td><td>recoup his strength<br>恢复体力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the long march the soldiers rested to recoup their strength.  长途行军后，士兵们休息以恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recover</td><td>/rɪˈkʌvər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>康复；恢复，找回</td><td>recover from illness<br>病后康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took him two months to recover from the serious operation.  他花了两个月才从这场大手术中康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 regain</td><td>/rɪˈɡeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新获得，恢复</td><td>regain consciousness<br>恢复意识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took him months to regain his strength after surgery.  手术后他花了几个月才恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repossess</td><td>/ˌriːpəˈzes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>收回，重新占有</td><td>repossess the car<br>收回汽车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank moved to repossess the house after six missed payments.  六次未还款后银行着手收回房屋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 retake</td><td>/ˌriːˈteɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重新夺取，收复</td><td>retake the hill<br>重新夺取山头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The commander ordered his soldiers to retake the bridge before dawn.  指挥官命令士兵在黎明前夺回那座桥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lose</td><td>/ˈluːz/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>失去；丢失</td><td>lose everything overnight<br>一夜间失去一切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless investors can lose everything overnight when a bubble finally bursts.  泡沫破裂时，粗心的投资者可能一夜之间失去一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 mislay</del></td><td>/mɪsˈleɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>把…放错地方而找不到</td><td>mislay one's glasses<br>把眼镜放错找不到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I seem to have mislaid my passport somewhere in the house.  我好像把护照放在家里某处找不到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 misplace</td><td>/mɪsˈpleɪs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放错地方，一时遗失</td><td>misplace the file<br>把文件放错地方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He misplaced the tickets and had to buy new ones.  他把票放错了地方，只好重新买。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>reverberate</b></td><td>/rɪˈvɜːrbəreɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>回荡，回响</td><td>reverberate through the hall<br>在大厅里回响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thunder reverberated through the narrow mountain valley for several minutes.  雷声在狭窄的山谷中回荡了好几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
